     1→// Package geminicli implements the Agent interface for Gemini CLI.
     2→package geminicli
     3→
     4→import (
     5→	"context"
     6→	"encoding/json"
     7→	"errors"
     8→	"fmt"
     9→	"io"
    10→	"log/slog"
    11→	"os"
    12→	"path/filepath"
    13→	"regexp"
    14→	"time"
    15→
    16→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    17→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging"
    18→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    19→)
    20→
    21→//nolint:gochecknoinits // Agent self-registration is the intended pattern
    22→func init() {
    23→	agent.Register(agent.AgentNameGemini, NewGeminiCLIAgent)
    24→}
    25→
    26→// GeminiCLIAgent implements the Agent interface for Gemini CLI.
    27→//
    28→//nolint:revive // GeminiCLIAgent is clearer than Agent in this context
    29→type GeminiCLIAgent struct{}
    30→
    31→func NewGeminiCLIAgent() agent.Agent {
    32→	return &GeminiCLIAgent{}
    33→}
    34→
    35→// Name returns the agent registry key.
    36→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) Name() agent.AgentName {
    37→	return agent.AgentNameGemini
    38→}
    39→
    40→// Type returns the agent type identifier.
    41→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) Type() agent.AgentType {
    42→	return agent.AgentTypeGemini
    43→}
    44→
    45→// Description returns a human-readable description.
    46→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) Description() string {
    47→	return "Gemini CLI - Google's AI coding assistant"
    48→}
    49→
    50→// DetectPresence checks if Gemini CLI is configured in the repository.
    51→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) DetectPresence() (bool, error) {
    52→	// Get repo root to check for .gemini directory
    53→	// This is needed because the CLI may be run from a subdirectory
    54→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    55→	if err != nil {
    56→		// Not in a git repo, fall back to CWD-relative check
    57→		repoRoot = "."
    58→	}
    59→
    60→	// Check for .gemini directory
    61→	geminiDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".gemini")
    62→	if _, err := os.Stat(geminiDir); err == nil {
    63→		return true, nil
    64→	}
    65→	// Check for .gemini/settings.json
    66→	settingsFile := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".gemini", "settings.json")
    67→	if _, err := os.Stat(settingsFile); err == nil {
    68→		return true, nil
    69→	}
    70→	return false, nil
    71→}
    72→
    73→// GetHookConfigPath returns the path to Gemini's hook config file.
    74→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetHookConfigPath() string {
    75→	return ".gemini/settings.json"
    76→}
    77→
    78→// SupportsHooks returns true as Gemini CLI supports lifecycle hooks.
    79→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) SupportsHooks() bool {
    80→	return true
    81→}
    82→
    83→// ParseHookInput parses Gemini CLI hook input from stdin.
    84→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ParseHookInput(hookType agent.HookType, reader io.Reader) (*agent.HookInput, error) {
    85→	data, err := io.ReadAll(reader)
    86→	if err != nil {
    87→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read input: %w", err)
    88→	}
    89→
    90→	if len(data) == 0 {
    91→		return nil, errors.New("empty input")
    92→	}
    93→
    94→	input := &agent.HookInput{
    95→		HookType:  hookType,
    96→		Timestamp: time.Now(),
    97→		RawData:   make(map[string]interface{}),
    98→	}
    99→
   100→	// Parse based on hook type
   101→	switch hookType {
   102→	case agent.HookSessionStart, agent.HookSessionEnd, agent.HookStop:
   103→		var raw sessionInfoRaw
   104→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   105→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse session info: %w", err)
   106→		}
   107→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   108→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   109→		// Store Gemini-specific fields in RawData
   110→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   111→		input.RawData["hook_event_name"] = raw.HookEventName
   112→		if raw.Source != "" {
   113→			input.RawData["source"] = raw.Source
   114→		}
   115→		if raw.Reason != "" {
   116→			input.RawData["reason"] = raw.Reason
   117→		}
   118→
   119→	case agent.HookUserPromptSubmit:
   120→		// BeforeAgent is Gemini's equivalent to Claude's UserPromptSubmit
   121→		// It provides the user's prompt in the "prompt" field
   122→		var raw agentHookInputRaw
   123→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   124→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse agent hook input: %w", err)
   125→		}
   126→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   127→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   128→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   129→		input.RawData["hook_event_name"] = raw.HookEventName
   130→		if raw.Prompt != "" {
   131→			input.UserPrompt = raw.Prompt
   132→			input.RawData["prompt"] = raw.Prompt
   133→		}
   134→
   135→	case agent.HookPreToolUse, agent.HookPostToolUse:
   136→		var raw toolHookInputRaw
   137→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   138→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse tool hook input: %w", err)
   139→		}
   140→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   141→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   142→		input.ToolName = raw.ToolName
   143→		input.ToolInput = raw.ToolInput
   144→		if hookType == agent.HookPostToolUse {
   145→			input.ToolResponse = raw.ToolResponse
   146→		}
   147→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   148→		input.RawData["hook_event_name"] = raw.HookEventName
   149→	}
   150→
   151→	return input, nil
   152→}
   153→
   154→// GetSessionID extracts the session ID from hook input.
   155→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetSessionID(input *agent.HookInput) string {
   156→	return input.SessionID
   157→}
   158→
   159→// ProtectedDirs returns directories that Gemini uses for config/state.
   160→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ProtectedDirs() []string { return []string{".gemini"} }
   161→
   162→// ResolveSessionFile returns the path to a Gemini session file.
   163→// Gemini names files as session-<date>-<shortid>.json where shortid is the first 8 chars
   164→// of the session UUID. This searches for an existing file matching the pattern, falling
   165→// back to <dir>/<id>.json if no match is found.
   166→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string {
   167→	// Try to find existing file matching Gemini's naming convention:
   168→	// session-*-<first8chars>.json
   169→	shortID := agentSessionID
   170→	if len(shortID) > 8 {
   171→		shortID = shortID[:8]
   172→	}
   173→
   174→	pattern := filepath.Join(sessionDir, "session-*-"+shortID+".json")
   175→	matches, err := filepath.Glob(pattern)
   176→	if err == nil && len(matches) > 0 {
   177→		// Return the most recent match (last alphabetically, since date is in the name)
   178→		return matches[len(matches)-1]
   179→	}
   180→
   181→	// Fallback: construct a default path
   182→	return filepath.Join(sessionDir, agentSessionID+".json")
   183→}
   184→
   185→// GetSessionDir returns the directory where Gemini stores session transcripts.
   186→// Gemini stores sessions in ~/.gemini/tmp/<project-hash>/chats/
   187→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {
   188→	// Check for test environment override
   189→	if override := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR"); override != "" {
   190→		return override, nil
   191→	}
   192→
   193→	homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   194→	if err != nil {
   195→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get home directory: %w", err)
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	// Gemini uses a hash of the project path for the directory name
   199→	projectDir := SanitizePathForGemini(repoPath)
   200→	return filepath.Join(homeDir, ".gemini", "tmp", projectDir, "chats"), nil
   201→}
   202→
   203→// ReadSession reads a session from Gemini's storage (JSON transcript file).
   204→// The session data is stored in NativeData as raw JSON bytes.
   205→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ReadSession(input *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   206→	if input.SessionRef == "" {
   207→		return nil, errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   208→	}
   209→
   210→	// Read the raw JSON file
   211→	data, err := os.ReadFile(input.SessionRef)
   212→	if err != nil {
   213→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   214→	}
   215→
   216→	// Parse to extract computed fields
   217→	modifiedFiles, err := ExtractModifiedFiles(data)
   218→	if err != nil {
   219→		// Non-fatal: we can still return the session without modified files
   220→		modifiedFiles = nil
   221→	}
   222→
   223→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   224→		SessionID:     input.SessionID,
   225→		AgentName:     g.Name(),
   226→		SessionRef:    input.SessionRef,
   227→		StartTime:     time.Now(),
   228→		NativeData:    data,
   229→		ModifiedFiles: modifiedFiles,
   230→	}, nil
   231→}
   232→
   233→// WriteSession writes a session to Gemini's storage (JSON transcript file).
   234→// Uses the NativeData field which contains raw JSON bytes.
   235→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) WriteSession(session *agent.AgentSession) error {
   236→	if session == nil {
   237→		return errors.New("session is nil")
   238→	}
   239→
   240→	// Verify this session belongs to Gemini CLI
   241→	if session.AgentName != "" && session.AgentName != g.Name() {
   242→		return fmt.Errorf("session belongs to agent %q, not %q", session.AgentName, g.Name())
   243→	}
   244→
   245→	if session.SessionRef == "" {
   246→		return errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   247→	}
   248→
   249→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   250→		return errors.New("session has no native data to write")
   251→	}
   252→
   253→	// Write the raw JSON data
   254→	if err := os.WriteFile(session.SessionRef, session.NativeData, 0o600); err != nil {
   255→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   256→	}
   257→
   258→	return nil
   259→}
   260→
   261→// FormatResumeCommand returns the command to resume a Gemini CLI session.
   262→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string {
   263→	return "gemini --resume " + sessionID
   264→}
   265→
   266→// SanitizePathForGemini converts a path to Gemini's project directory format.
   267→// Gemini uses a hash-like sanitization similar to Claude.
   268→var nonAlphanumericRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`[^a-zA-Z0-9]`)
   269→
   270→func SanitizePathForGemini(path string) string {
   271→	return nonAlphanumericRegex.ReplaceAllString(path, "-")
   272→}
   273→
   274→// TranscriptAnalyzer interface implementation
   275→
   276→// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current message count of a Gemini transcript.
   277→// Gemini uses JSON format with a messages array, so position is the message count.
   278→// Returns 0 if the file doesn't exist or is empty.
   279→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
   280→	if path == "" {
   281→		return 0, nil
   282→	}
   283→
   284→	data, err := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   285→	if err != nil {
   286→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   287→			return 0, nil
   288→		}
   289→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   290→	}
   291→
   292→	if len(data) == 0 {
   293→		return 0, nil
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	transcript, err := ParseTranscript(data)
   297→	if err != nil {
   298→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   299→	}
   300→
   301→	return len(transcript.Messages), nil
   302→}
   303→
   304→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given message index.
   305→// For Gemini (JSON format), offset is the starting message index.
   306→// Returns:
   307→//   - files: list of file paths modified by Gemini (from Write/Edit tools)
   308→//   - currentPosition: total number of messages in the transcript
   309→//   - error: any error encountered during reading
   310→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error) {
   311→	if path == "" {
   312→		return nil, 0, nil
   313→	}
   314→
   315→	data, readErr := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   316→	if readErr != nil {
   317→		if os.IsNotExist(readErr) {
   318→			return nil, 0, nil
   319→		}
   320→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", readErr)
   321→	}
   322→
   323→	if len(data) == 0 {
   324→		return nil, 0, nil
   325→	}
   326→
   327→	transcript, parseErr := ParseTranscript(data)
   328→	if parseErr != nil {
   329→		return nil, 0, parseErr
   330→	}
   331→
   332→	totalMessages := len(transcript.Messages)
   333→
   334→	// Extract files from messages starting at startOffset
   335→	fileSet := make(map[string]bool)
   336→	for i := startOffset; i < len(transcript.Messages); i++ {
   337→		msg := transcript.Messages[i]
   338→		// Only process gemini messages (assistant messages)
   339→		if msg.Type != MessageTypeGemini {
   340→			continue
   341→		}
   342→
   343→		// Process tool calls in this message
   344→		for _, toolCall := range msg.ToolCalls {
   345→			// Check if it's a file modification tool
   346→			isModifyTool := false
   347→			for _, name := range FileModificationTools {
   348→				if toolCall.Name == name {
   349→					isModifyTool = true
   350→					break
   351→				}
   352→			}
   353→
   354→			if !isModifyTool {
   355→				continue
   356→			}
   357→
   358→			// Extract file path from args map
   359→			var file string
   360→			if fp, ok := toolCall.Args["file_path"].(string); ok && fp != "" {
   361→				file = fp
   362→			} else if p, ok := toolCall.Args["path"].(string); ok && p != "" {
   363→				file = p
   364→			} else if fn, ok := toolCall.Args["filename"].(string); ok && fn != "" {
   365→				file = fn
   366→			}
   367→
   368→			if file != "" && !fileSet[file] {
   369→				fileSet[file] = true
   370→				files = append(files, file)
   371→			}
   372→		}
   373→	}
   374→
   375→	return files, totalMessages, nil
   376→}
   377→
   378→// ChunkTranscript splits a Gemini JSON transcript by distributing messages across chunks.
   379→// Gemini uses JSON format with a {"messages": [...]} structure, so chunking splits
   380→// the messages array while preserving the JSON structure in each chunk.
   381→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
   382→	var transcript GeminiTranscript
   383→	if err := json.Unmarshal(content, &transcript); err != nil {
   384→		// Fall back to JSONL chunking if not valid Gemini JSON
   385→		chunks, chunkErr := agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   386→		if chunkErr != nil {
   387→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk as JSONL: %w", chunkErr)
   388→		}
   389→		return chunks, nil
   390→	}
   391→
   392→	if len(transcript.Messages) == 0 {
   393→		return [][]byte{content}, nil
   394→	}
   395→
   396→	var chunks [][]byte
   397→	var currentMessages []GeminiMessage
   398→	currentSize := len(`{"messages":[]}`) // Base JSON structure size
   399→
   400→	for i, msg := range transcript.Messages {
   401→		// Marshal message to get its size
   402→		msgBytes, err := json.Marshal(msg)
   403→		if err != nil {
   404→			logging.Warn(context.Background(), "failed to marshal Gemini message during chunking",
   405→				slog.Int("message_index", i),
   406→				slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   407→			)
   408→			continue
   409→		}
   410→		msgSize := len(msgBytes) + 1 // +1 for comma separator
   411→
   412→		if currentSize+msgSize > maxSize && len(currentMessages) > 0 {
   413→			// Save current chunk
   414→			chunkData, err := json.Marshal(GeminiTranscript{Messages: currentMessages})
   415→			if err != nil {
   416→				return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal chunk: %w", err)
   417→			}
   418→			chunks = append(chunks, chunkData)
   419→
   420→			// Start new chunk
   421→			currentMessages = nil
   422→			currentSize = len(`{"messages":[]}`)
   423→		}
   424→
   425→		currentMessages = append(currentMessages, msg)
   426→		currentSize += msgSize
   427→	}
   428→
   429→	// Add the last chunk
   430→	if len(currentMessages) > 0 {
   431→		chunkData, err := json.Marshal(GeminiTranscript{Messages: currentMessages})
   432→		if err != nil {
   433→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal final chunk: %w", err)
   434→		}
   435→		chunks = append(chunks, chunkData)
   436→	}
   437→
   438→	// Ensure we created at least one chunk (could be empty if all messages failed to marshal)
   439→	if len(chunks) == 0 {
   440→		return nil, errors.New("failed to create any chunks: all messages failed to marshal")
   441→	}
   442→
   443→	return chunks, nil
   444→}
   445→
   446→// ReassembleTranscript merges Gemini JSON chunks by combining their message arrays.
   447→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
   448→	var allMessages []GeminiMessage
   449→
   450→	for _, chunk := range chunks {
   451→		var transcript GeminiTranscript
   452→		if err := json.Unmarshal(chunk, &transcript); err != nil {
   453→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal chunk: %w", err)
   454→		}
   455→		allMessages = append(allMessages, transcript.Messages...)
   456→	}
   457→
   458→	result, err := json.Marshal(GeminiTranscript{Messages: allMessages})
   459→	if err != nil {
   460→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal reassembled transcript: %w", err)
   461→	}
   462→	return result, nil
   463→}

    62→// InstallHooks installs Gemini CLI hooks in .gemini/settings.json.
    63→// If force is true, removes existing Entire hooks before installing.
    64→// Returns the number of hooks installed.
    65→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error) {
    66→	// Use repo root instead of CWD to find .gemini directory
    67→	// This ensures hooks are installed correctly when run from a subdirectory
    68→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    69→	if err != nil {
    70→		// Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo (e.g., during tests)
    71→		repoRoot, err = os.Getwd() //nolint:forbidigo // Intentional fallback when RepoRoot() fails (tests run outside git repos)
    72→		if err != nil {
    73→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to get current directory: %w", err)
    74→		}
    75→	}
    76→
    77→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".gemini", GeminiSettingsFileName)
    78→
    79→	// Read existing settings if they exist
    80→	var rawSettings map[string]json.RawMessage
    81→
    82→	// rawHooks preserves unknown hook types
    83→	var rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage
    84→
    85→	var hooksConfig GeminiHooksConfig
    86→
    87→	existingData, readErr := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from cwd + fixed path
    88→	if readErr == nil {
    89→		if err := json.Unmarshal(existingData, &rawSettings); err != nil {
    90→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse existing settings.json: %w", err)
    91→		}
    92→		if hooksRaw, ok := rawSettings["hooks"]; ok {
    93→			if err := json.Unmarshal(hooksRaw, &rawHooks); err != nil {
    94→				return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hooks in settings.json: %w", err)
    95→			}
    96→		}
    97→		if hooksConfigRaw, ok := rawSettings["hooksConfig"]; ok {
    98→			if err := json.Unmarshal(hooksConfigRaw, &hooksConfig); err != nil {
    99→				return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hooksConfig in settings.json: %w", err)
   100→			}
   101→		}
   102→	} else {
   103→		rawSettings = make(map[string]json.RawMessage)
   104→	}
   105→
   106→	if rawHooks == nil {
   107→		rawHooks = make(map[string]json.RawMessage)
   108→	}
   109→
   110→	// Enable hooks via hooksConfig
   111→	// hooksConfig.Enabled must be true for Gemini CLI to execute hooks
   112→	hooksConfig.Enabled = true
   113→
   114→	// Define hook commands based on localDev mode
   115→	var cmdPrefix string
   116→	if localDev {
   117→		cmdPrefix = "go run ${GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks gemini "
   118→	} else {
   119→		cmdPrefix = "entire hooks gemini "
   120→	}
   121→
   122→	// Parse only the hook types we need to modify
   123→	var sessionStart, sessionEnd, beforeAgent, afterAgent []GeminiHookMatcher
   124→	var beforeModel, afterModel, beforeToolSelection []GeminiHookMatcher
   125→	var beforeTool, afterTool, preCompress, notification []GeminiHookMatcher
   126→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "SessionStart", &sessionStart)
   127→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "SessionEnd", &sessionEnd)
   128→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeAgent", &beforeAgent)
   129→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "AfterAgent", &afterAgent)
   130→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeModel", &beforeModel)

   134→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "AfterTool", &afterTool)
   135→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "PreCompress", &preCompress)
   136→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "Notification", &notification)
   137→
   138→	// Check for idempotency BEFORE removing hooks
   139→	// If the exact same hook command already exists, return 0 (no changes needed)
   140→	if !force {
   141→		existingCmd := getFirstEntireHookCommand(sessionStart)
   142→		expectedCmd := cmdPrefix + "session-start"
   143→		if existingCmd == expectedCmd {
   144→			return 0, nil // Already installed with same mode
   145→		}
   146→	}
   147→
   148→	// Remove existing Entire hooks first (for clean installs and mode switching)
   149→	sessionStart = removeEntireHooks(sessionStart)

   160→
   161→	// Install all hooks

   163→	sessionStart = addGeminiHook(sessionStart, "", "entire-session-start", cmdPrefix+"session-start")
   164→	// SessionEnd fires on both "exit" and "logout" - install hooks for both matchers
   165→	sessionEnd = addGeminiHook(sessionEnd, "exit", "entire-session-end-exit", cmdPrefix+"session-end")

   170→	afterAgent = addGeminiHook(afterAgent, "", "entire-after-agent", cmdPrefix+"after-agent")

   174→	afterModel = addGeminiHook(afterModel, "", "entire-after-model", cmdPrefix+"after-model")
   175→
   176→	// Tool selection hook (before planner selects tools)
   177→	beforeToolSelection = addGeminiHook(beforeToolSelection, "", "entire-before-tool-selection", cmdPrefix+"before-tool-selection")
   178→
   179→	// Tool hooks (before/after tool execution)
   180→	beforeTool = addGeminiHook(beforeTool, "*", "entire-before-tool", cmdPrefix+"before-tool")
   181→	afterTool = addGeminiHook(afterTool, "*", "entire-after-tool", cmdPrefix+"after-tool")
   182→
   183→	// Compression hook (before chat history compression)
   184→	preCompress = addGeminiHook(preCompress, "", "entire-pre-compress", cmdPrefix+"pre-compress")
   185→
   186→	// Notification hook (errors, warnings, info)
   187→	notification = addGeminiHook(notification, "", "entire-notification", cmdPrefix+"notification")
   188→
   189→	// 12 hooks total:
   190→	// - session-start (1)
   191→	// - session-end exit + logout (2)
   192→	// - before-agent, after-agent (2)
   193→	// - before-model, after-model (2)
   194→	// - before-tool-selection (1)
   195→	// - before-tool, after-tool (2)
   196→	// - pre-compress (1)
   197→	// - notification (1)
   198→	count := 12
   199→
   200→	// Marshal modified hook types back to rawHooks
   201→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "SessionStart", sessionStart)
   202→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "SessionEnd", sessionEnd)
   203→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeAgent", beforeAgent)
   204→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "AfterAgent", afterAgent)
   205→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeModel", beforeModel)
   206→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "AfterModel", afterModel)
   207→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeToolSelection", beforeToolSelection)
   208→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeTool", beforeTool)
   209→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "AfterTool", afterTool)
   210→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "PreCompress", preCompress)
   211→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "Notification", notification)
   212→
   213→	// Marshal hooksConfig back to raw settings
   214→	hooksConfigJSON, err := json.Marshal(hooksConfig)
   215→	if err != nil {
   216→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal hooksConfig: %w", err)
   217→	}
   218→	rawSettings["hooksConfig"] = hooksConfigJSON
   219→
   220→	// Marshal hooks back to raw settings (preserving unknown hook types)
   221→	hooksJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawHooks)
   222→	if err != nil {
   223→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal hooks: %w", err)
   224→	}
   225→	rawSettings["hooks"] = hooksJSON
   226→
   227→	// Write back to file
   228→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(settingsPath), 0o750); err != nil {
   229→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create .gemini directory: %w", err)
   230→	}
   231→
   232→	output, err := json.MarshalIndent(rawSettings, "", "  ")
   233→	if err != nil {
   234→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal settings: %w", err)
   235→	}
   236→
   237→	if err := os.WriteFile(settingsPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {
   238→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to write settings.json: %w", err)
   239→	}
   240→
   241→	return count, nil
   242→}
   243→
   244→// parseGeminiHookType parses a specific hook type from rawHooks into the target slice.
   245→// Silently ignores parse errors (leaves target unchanged).
   246→func parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage, hookType string, target *[]GeminiHookMatcher) {
   247→	if data, ok := rawHooks[hookType]; ok {
   248→		//nolint:errcheck,gosec // Intentionally ignoring parse errors - leave target as nil/empty
   249→		json.Unmarshal(data, target)
   250→	}
   251→}
   252→
   253→// marshalGeminiHookType marshals a hook type back to rawHooks.
   254→// If the slice is empty, removes the key from rawHooks.
   255→func marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage, hookType string, matchers []GeminiHookMatcher) {
   256→	if len(matchers) == 0 {
   257→		delete(rawHooks, hookType)
   258→		return
   259→	}
   260→	data, err := json.Marshal(matchers)
   261→	if err != nil {
   262→		return // Silently ignore marshal errors (shouldn't happen)
   263→	}
   264→	rawHooks[hookType] = data
   265→}
   266→
   267→// UninstallHooks removes Entire hooks from Gemini CLI settings.
   268→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) UninstallHooks() error {
   269→	// Use repo root to find .gemini directory when run from a subdirectory
   270→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   271→	if err != nil {
   272→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo
   273→	}
   274→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".gemini", GeminiSettingsFileName)
   275→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   276→	if err != nil {
   277→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // No settings file means nothing to uninstall
   278→	}
   279→
   280→	var rawSettings map[string]json.RawMessage
   281→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &rawSettings); err != nil {
   282→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse settings.json: %w", err)
   283→	}
   284→
   285→	// rawHooks preserves unknown hook types
   286→	var rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage
   287→	if hooksRaw, ok := rawSettings["hooks"]; ok {
   288→		if err := json.Unmarshal(hooksRaw, &rawHooks); err != nil {
   289→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hooks: %w", err)
   290→		}
   291→	}
   292→	if rawHooks == nil {
   293→		rawHooks = make(map[string]json.RawMessage)
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	// Parse only the hook types we need to modify
   297→	var sessionStart, sessionEnd, beforeAgent, afterAgent []GeminiHookMatcher
   298→	var beforeModel, afterModel, beforeToolSelection []GeminiHookMatcher
   299→	var beforeTool, afterTool, preCompress, notification []GeminiHookMatcher
   300→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "SessionStart", &sessionStart)
   301→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "SessionEnd", &sessionEnd)
   302→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeAgent", &beforeAgent)
   303→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "AfterAgent", &afterAgent)
   304→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeModel", &beforeModel)
   305→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "AfterModel", &afterModel)
   306→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeToolSelection", &beforeToolSelection)
   307→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeTool", &beforeTool)
   308→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "AfterTool", &afterTool)
   309→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "PreCompress", &preCompress)
   310→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "Notification", &notification)
   311→
   312→	// Remove Entire hooks from all hook types
   313→	sessionStart = removeEntireHooks(sessionStart)
   314→	sessionEnd = removeEntireHooks(sessionEnd)
   315→	beforeAgent = removeEntireHooks(beforeAgent)
   316→	afterAgent = removeEntireHooks(afterAgent)
   317→	beforeModel = removeEntireHooks(beforeModel)
   318→	afterModel = removeEntireHooks(afterModel)
   319→	beforeToolSelection = removeEntireHooks(beforeToolSelection)
   320→	beforeTool = removeEntireHooks(beforeTool)
   321→	afterTool = removeEntireHooks(afterTool)
   322→	preCompress = removeEntireHooks(preCompress)
   323→	notification = removeEntireHooks(notification)
   324→
   325→	// Marshal modified hook types back to rawHooks
   326→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "SessionStart", sessionStart)
   327→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "SessionEnd", sessionEnd)
   328→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeAgent", beforeAgent)
   329→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "AfterAgent", afterAgent)
   330→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeModel", beforeModel)
   331→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "AfterModel", afterModel)
   332→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeToolSelection", beforeToolSelection)
   333→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeTool", beforeTool)
   334→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "AfterTool", afterTool)
   335→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "PreCompress", preCompress)
   336→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "Notification", notification)
   337→
   338→	// Marshal hooks back (preserving unknown hook types)
   339→	if len(rawHooks) > 0 {
   340→		hooksJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawHooks)
   341→		if err != nil {
   342→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal hooks: %w", err)
   343→		}
   344→		rawSettings["hooks"] = hooksJSON
   345→	} else {
   346→		delete(rawSettings, "hooks")
   347→	}
   348→
   349→	// Write back
   350→	output, err := json.MarshalIndent(rawSettings, "", "  ")
   351→	if err != nil {
   352→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal settings: %w", err)
   353→	}
   354→
   355→	if err := os.WriteFile(settingsPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {
   356→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write settings.json: %w", err)
   357→	}
   358→	return nil
   359→}
   360→
   361→// AreHooksInstalled checks if Entire hooks are installed.
   362→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) AreHooksInstalled() bool {
   363→	// Use repo root to find .gemini directory when run from a subdirectory
   364→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   365→	if err != nil {
   366→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo
   367→	}
   368→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".gemini", GeminiSettingsFileName)
   369→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   370→	if err != nil {
   371→		return false
   372→	}
   373→
   374→	var settings GeminiSettings
   375→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &settings); err != nil {
   376→		return false
   377→	}
   378→
   379→	// Check for at least one of our hooks using isEntireHook (works for both localDev and production)
   380→	return hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.SessionStart) ||
   381→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.SessionEnd) ||
   382→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.BeforeAgent) ||
   383→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.AfterAgent) ||
   384→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.BeforeModel) ||
   385→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.AfterModel) ||
   386→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.BeforeToolSelection) ||
   387→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.BeforeTool) ||
   388→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.AfterTool) ||
   389→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.PreCompress) ||
   390→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.Notification)
   391→}
   392→
   393→// GetSupportedHooks returns the hook types Gemini CLI supports.
   394→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetSupportedHooks() []agent.HookType {
   395→	return []agent.HookType{
   396→		agent.HookSessionStart,
   397→		agent.HookSessionEnd,       // Maps to Gemini's SessionEnd (explicit exit/logout)
   398→		agent.HookStop,             // Maps to Gemini's AfterAgent (end of response)
   399→		agent.HookUserPromptSubmit, // Maps to Gemini's BeforeAgent (user prompt)
   400→		agent.HookPreToolUse,       // Maps to Gemini's BeforeTool
   401→		agent.HookPostToolUse,      // Maps to Gemini's AfterTool
   402→	}
   403→}
   404→
   405→// Helper functions for hook management
   406→
   407→// addGeminiHook adds a hook entry to matchers.
   408→// Unlike Claude Code, Gemini hooks require a "name" field.
   409→func addGeminiHook(matchers []GeminiHookMatcher, matcherName, hookName, command string) []GeminiHookMatcher {
   410→	entry := GeminiHookEntry{
   411→		Name:    hookName,
   412→		Type:    "command",
   413→		Command: command,
   414→	}
   415→
   416→	// Find or create matcher
   417→	for i, matcher := range matchers {
   418→		if matcher.Matcher == matcherName {
   419→			matchers[i].Hooks = append(matchers[i].Hooks, entry)
   420→			return matchers
   421→		}
   422→	}
   423→
   424→	// Create new matcher
   425→	newMatcher := GeminiHookMatcher{
   426→		Hooks: []GeminiHookEntry{entry},
   427→	}
   428→	if matcherName != "" {
   429→		newMatcher.Matcher = matcherName
   430→	}
   431→	return append(matchers, newMatcher)
   432→}
   433→
   434→// isEntireHook checks if a command is an Entire hook
   435→func isEntireHook(command string) bool {
   436→	for _, prefix := range entireHookPrefixes {
   437→		if strings.HasPrefix(command, prefix) {
   438→			return true
   439→		}
   440→	}
   441→	return false
   442→}
   443→
   444→// hasEntireHook checks if any hook in the matchers is an Entire hook
   445→func hasEntireHook(matchers []GeminiHookMatcher) bool {
   446→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   447→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   448→			if isEntireHook(hook.Command) {
   449→				return true
   450→			}
   451→		}
   452→	}
   453→	return false
   454→}
   455→
   456→// getFirstEntireHookCommand returns the command of the first Entire hook found, or empty string
   457→func getFirstEntireHookCommand(matchers []GeminiHookMatcher) string {
   458→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   459→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   460→			if isEntireHook(hook.Command) {
   461→				return hook.Command
   462→			}
   463→		}
   464→	}
   465→	return ""
   466→}
   467→
   468→// removeEntireHooks removes all Entire hooks from a list of matchers
   469→func removeEntireHooks(matchers []GeminiHookMatcher) []GeminiHookMatcher {
   470→	result := make([]GeminiHookMatcher, 0, len(matchers))
   471→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   472→		filteredHooks := make([]GeminiHookEntry, 0, len(matcher.Hooks))
   473→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   474→			if !isEntireHook(hook.Command) {
   475→				filteredHooks = append(filteredHooks, hook)
   476→			}
   477→		}
   478→		// Only keep the matcher if it has hooks remaining
   479→		if len(filteredHooks) > 0 {
   480→			matcher.Hooks = filteredHooks
   481→			result = append(result, matcher)
   482→		}
   483→	}
   484→	return result
   485→}

   163→func newDisableCmd() *cobra.Command {
   164→	var useProjectSettings bool
   165→	var uninstall bool
   166→	var force bool
   167→
   168→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
   169→		Use:   "disable",
   170→		Short: "Disable Entire in current project",
   171→		Long: `Disable Entire integrations in the current project.
   172→
   173→By default, this command will disable Entire. Hooks will exit silently and commands will
   174→show a disabled message.
   175→
   176→To completely remove Entire integrations from this repository, use --uninstall:
   177→  - .entire/ directory (settings, logs, metadata)
   178→  - Git hooks (prepare-commit-msg, commit-msg, post-commit, pre-push)
   179→  - Session state files (.git/entire-sessions/)
   180→  - Shadow branches (entire/<hash>)
   181→  - Agent hooks (Claude Code, Gemini CLI)`,
   182→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   183→			if uninstall {
   184→				return runUninstall(cmd.OutOrStdout(), cmd.ErrOrStderr(), force)
   185→			}
   186→			return runDisable(cmd.OutOrStdout(), useProjectSettings)
   187→		},
   188→	}
   189→
   190→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&useProjectSettings, "project", false, "Update .entire/settings.json instead of .entire/settings.local.json")
   191→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&uninstall, "uninstall", false, "Completely remove Entire from this repository")
   192→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&force, "force", false, "Skip confirmation prompt (use with --uninstall)")
   193→
   194→	return cmd
   195→}
   196→
   197→// isFullyEnabled checks whether Entire is already fully set up.
   198→// Returns whether it's fully enabled, and if so, the agent type display name and config file path.
   199→func isFullyEnabled() (enabled bool, agentDesc string, configPath string) {
   200→	// Check settings exist and Enabled == true
   201→	s, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   202→	if err != nil || !s.Enabled {
   203→		return false, "", ""
   204→	}
   205→
   206→	// Check any agent hooks installed (not just Claude Code — works with Gemini too)
   207→	installedAgents := GetAgentsWithHooksInstalled()
   208→	if len(installedAgents) == 0 {
   209→		return false, "", ""
   210→	}
   211→
   212→	// Check git hooks installed
   213→	if !strategy.IsGitHookInstalled() {
   214→		return false, "", ""
   215→	}
   216→
   217→	// Check .entire directory exists
   218→	if !checkEntireDirExists() {
   219→		return false, "", ""
   220→	}
   221→
   222→	// Determine agent description from first installed agent
   223→	desc := string(installedAgents[0]) // fallback to agent name
   224→	if ag, err := agent.Get(installedAgents[0]); err == nil {
   225→		desc = string(ag.Type())
   226→	}
   227→
   228→	// Determine config path - check if local settings exists, otherwise show project settings
   229→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
   230→	if err != nil {
   231→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir
   232→	}
   233→	configDisplay := configDisplayProject
   234→	localSettingsPath := filepath.Join(entireDirAbs, "settings.local.json")
   235→	if _, err := os.Stat(localSettingsPath); err == nil {
   236→		configDisplay = configDisplayLocal
   237→	}
   238→
   239→	return true, desc, configDisplay
   240→}
   241→
   242→// runEnableWithStrategy enables Entire with a specified strategy (non-interactive).
   243→// The selectedStrategy can be either a display name (manual-commit, auto-commit)
   244→// or an internal name (manual-commit, auto-commit).
   245→// agents must be provided by the caller (via detectOrSelectAgent).
   246→func runEnableWithStrategy(w io.Writer, agents []agent.Agent, selectedStrategy string, localDev, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry bool) error {
   247→	// Map the strategy to internal name if it's a display name
   248→	internalStrategy := selectedStrategy
   249→	if mapped, ok := strategyDisplayToInternal[selectedStrategy]; ok {
   250→		internalStrategy = mapped
   251→	}
   252→
   253→	// Validate the strategy exists
   254→	strat, err := strategy.Get(internalStrategy)
   255→	if err != nil {
   256→		return fmt.Errorf("unknown strategy: %s (use manual-commit or auto-commit)", selectedStrategy)
   257→	}
   258→
   259→	// Setup agent hooks for all selected agents
   260→	for _, ag := range agents {
   261→		if _, err := setupAgentHooks(ag, localDev, forceHooks); err != nil {
   262→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup %s hooks: %w", ag.Type(), err)
   263→		}
   264→	}
   265→
   266→	// Setup .entire directory
   267→	if _, err := setupEntireDirectory(); err != nil {
   268→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup .entire directory: %w", err)
   269→	}
   270→
   271→	// Load existing settings to preserve other options (like strategy_options.push)
   272→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   273→	if err != nil {
   274→		// If we can't load, start with defaults
   275→		settings = &EntireSettings{}
   276→	}
   277→	// Update the specific fields
   278→	settings.Strategy = internalStrategy
   279→	settings.LocalDev = localDev
   280→	settings.Enabled = true
   281→
   282→	// Set push_sessions option if --skip-push-sessions flag was provided
   283→	if skipPushSessions {
   284→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   285→			settings.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]interface{})
   286→		}
   287→		settings.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"] = false
   288→	}
   289→
   290→	// Handle telemetry for non-interactive mode
   291→	// Note: if telemetry is nil (not configured), it defaults to disabled
   292→	if !telemetry || os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT") != "" {
   293→		f := false
   294→		settings.Telemetry = &f
   295→	}
   296→
   297→	// Determine which settings file to write to
   298→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
   299→	if err != nil {
   300→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir // Fallback to relative
   301→	}
   302→	shouldUseLocal, showNotification := determineSettingsTarget(entireDirAbs, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings)
   303→
   304→	if showNotification {
   305→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "Info: Project settings exist. Saving to settings.local.json instead.")
   306→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Use --project to update the project settings file.")
   307→	}
   308→
   309→	configDisplay := configDisplayProject
   310→	if shouldUseLocal {
   311→		if err := SaveEntireSettingsLocal(settings); err != nil {
   312→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save local settings: %w", err)
   313→		}
   314→		configDisplay = configDisplayLocal
   315→	} else {
   316→		if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   317→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   318→		}
   319→	}
   320→
   321→	// Install git hooks AFTER saving settings (InstallGitHook reads local_dev from settings)
   322→	if _, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(true); err != nil {
   323→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hooks: %w", err)
   324→	}
   325→	strategy.CheckAndWarnHookManagers(w)
   326→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Hooks installed")
   327→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Project configured (%s)\n", configDisplay)
   328→
   329→	// Let the strategy handle its own setup requirements
   330→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   331→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup strategy: %w", err)
   332→	}
   333→
   334→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nReady.")
   335→
   336→	return nil
   337→}
   338→
   339→// runEnableInteractive runs the interactive enable flow.
   340→// agents must be provided by the caller (via detectOrSelectAgent).
   341→// The isFullyEnabled check is handled by the caller before agent detection.
   342→func runEnableInteractive(w io.Writer, agents []agent.Agent, localDev, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry bool) error {
   343→	// Setup agent hooks for all selected agents
   344→	for _, ag := range agents {
   345→		if _, err := setupAgentHooks(ag, localDev, forceHooks); err != nil {
   346→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup %s hooks: %w", ag.Type(), err)
   347→		}
   348→	}
   349→
   350→	// Setup .entire directory
   351→	if _, err := setupEntireDirectory(); err != nil {
   352→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup .entire directory: %w", err)
   353→	}
   354→
   355→	// Use the default strategy (manual-commit)
   356→	internalStrategy := strategy.DefaultStrategyName
   357→
   358→	// Load existing settings to preserve other options (like strategy_options.push)
   359→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   360→	if err != nil {
   361→		// If we can't load, start with defaults
   362→		settings = &EntireSettings{}
   363→	}
   364→	// Update the specific fields
   365→	settings.Strategy = internalStrategy
   366→	settings.LocalDev = localDev
   367→	settings.Enabled = true
   368→
   369→	// Set push_sessions option if --skip-push-sessions flag was provided
   370→	if skipPushSessions {
   371→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   372→			settings.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]interface{})
   373→		}
   374→		settings.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"] = false
   375→	}
   376→
   377→	// Determine which settings file to write to
   378→	// First run always creates settings.json (no prompt)
   379→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
   380→	if err != nil {
   381→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir // Fallback to relative
   382→	}
   383→	shouldUseLocal, showNotification := determineSettingsTarget(entireDirAbs, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings)
   384→
   385→	if showNotification {
   386→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "Info: Project settings exist. Saving to settings.local.json instead.")
   387→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Use --project to update the project settings file.")
   388→	}
   389→
   390→	// Helper to save settings to the appropriate file
   391→	saveSettings := func() error {
   392→		if shouldUseLocal {
   393→			return SaveEntireSettingsLocal(settings)
   394→		}
   395→		return SaveEntireSettings(settings)
   396→	}
   397→
   398→	// Save settings before telemetry prompt so config is persisted even if the user cancels
   399→	if err := saveSettings(); err != nil {
   400→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   401→	}
   402→
   403→	// Install git hooks AFTER saving settings (InstallGitHook reads local_dev from settings)
   404→	if _, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(true); err != nil {
   405→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hooks: %w", err)
   406→	}
   407→	strategy.CheckAndWarnHookManagers(w)
   408→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Hooks installed")
   409→
   410→	configDisplay := configDisplayProject
   411→	if shouldUseLocal {
   412→		configDisplay = configDisplayLocal
   413→	}
   414→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Project configured (%s)\n", configDisplay)
   415→
   416→	// Ask about telemetry consent (only if not already asked)
   417→	fmt.Fprintln(w)
   418→	if err := promptTelemetryConsent(settings, telemetry); err != nil {
   419→		return fmt.Errorf("telemetry consent: %w", err)
   420→	}
   421→	// Save again to persist telemetry choice
   422→	if err := saveSettings(); err != nil {
   423→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   424→	}
   425→
   426→	// Let the strategy handle its own setup requirements
   427→	strat, err := strategy.Get(internalStrategy)
   428→	if err != nil {
   429→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get strategy: %w", err)
   430→	}
   431→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   432→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup strategy: %w", err)
   433→	}
   434→
   435→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nReady.")
   436→
   437→	return nil
   438→}
   439→
   440→// runEnable is a simple enable that just sets the enabled flag (for programmatic use).
   441→func runEnable(w io.Writer) error {
   442→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   443→	if err != nil {
   444→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
   445→	}
   446→
   447→	settings.Enabled = true
   448→	if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   449→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   450→	}
   451→
   452→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Entire is now enabled.")
   453→	return nil
   454→}
   455→
   456→func runDisable(w io.Writer, useProjectSettings bool) error {
   457→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   458→	if err != nil {
   459→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
   460→	}
   461→
   462→	settings.Enabled = false
   463→
   464→	// If --project flag is specified, always write to project settings
   465→	if useProjectSettings {
   466→		if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   467→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   468→		}
   469→	} else {
   470→		// Always write to local settings file (create if doesn't exist)
   471→		if err := SaveEntireSettingsLocal(settings); err != nil {
   472→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save local settings: %w", err)
   473→		}
   474→	}
   475→
   476→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Entire is now disabled.")
   477→	return nil
   478→}
   479→
   480→// DisabledMessage is the message shown when Entire is disabled
   481→const DisabledMessage = "Entire is disabled. Run `entire enable` to re-enable."
   482→
   483→// checkDisabledGuard checks if Entire is disabled and prints a message if so.
   484→// Returns true if the caller should exit (i.e., Entire is disabled).
   485→// On error reading settings, defaults to enabled (returns false).
   486→func checkDisabledGuard(w io.Writer) bool {
   487→	enabled, err := IsEnabled()
   488→	if err != nil {
   489→		// Default to enabled on error
   490→		return false
   491→	}
   492→	if !enabled {
   493→		fmt.Fprintln(w, DisabledMessage)
   494→		return true
   495→	}
   496→	return false
   497→}
   498→
   499→// setupAgentHooks sets up hooks for a given agent.
   500→// Returns the number of hooks installed (0 if already installed).
   501→func setupAgentHooks(ag agent.Agent, localDev, forceHooks bool) (int, error) { //nolint:unparam // return value used by setupAgentHooksNonInteractive
   502→	hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
   503→	if !ok {
   504→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("agent %s does not support hooks", ag.Name())
   505→	}
   506→
   507→	count, err := hookAgent.InstallHooks(localDev, forceHooks)
   508→	if err != nil {
   509→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to install %s hooks: %w", ag.Name(), err)
   510→	}
   511→
   512→	return count, nil
   513→}
   514→
   515→// detectOrSelectAgent tries to auto-detect agents, or prompts the user to select.
   516→// Returns the detected/selected agents and any error.
   517→// When exactly one agent is detected, it is used automatically.
   518→// When multiple agents are detected, the user is prompted to confirm.
   519→// If no agent is detected and no TTY is available, falls back to the default agent.
   520→//
   521→// selectFn overrides the interactive prompt for testing. When nil, the real form is used.
   522→// It receives available agent names and returns the selected names.
   523→func detectOrSelectAgent(w io.Writer, selectFn func(available []string) ([]string, error)) ([]agent.Agent, error) {
   524→	// Try auto-detection first
   525→	detected := agent.DetectAll()
   526→
   527→	switch {
   528→	case len(detected) == 1:
   529→		// Single agent detected — use it directly
   530→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "Detected agent: %s\n\n", detected[0].Type())
   531→		return detected, nil
   532→
   533→	case len(detected) > 1:
   534→		// Multiple agents detected — prompt the user to confirm which to enable
   535→		agentTypes := make([]string, 0, len(detected))
   536→		for _, ag := range detected {
   537→			agentTypes = append(agentTypes, string(ag.Type()))
   538→		}
   539→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "Detected multiple agents: %s\n", strings.Join(agentTypes, ", "))
   540→		fmt.Fprintln(w)
   541→		// Fall through to the interactive multi-select below
   542→	}
   543→
   544→	// No agent detected (or multiple detected) — check if we can prompt interactively
   545→	if !canPromptInteractively() {
   546→		if len(detected) > 0 {
   547→			// Multiple agents detected but no TTY — use all of them
   548→			return detected, nil
   549→		}
   550→		// No TTY available (e.g., running in CI or tests) - fall back to default agent
   551→		defaultAgent := agent.Default()
   552→		if defaultAgent == nil {
   553→			return nil, errors.New("no default agent available")
   554→		}
   555→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "Agent: %s (use --agent to change)\n\n", defaultAgent.Type())
   556→		return []agent.Agent{defaultAgent}, nil
   557→	}
   558→
   559→	if len(detected) == 0 {
   560→		// Show message only when nothing was detected
   561→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "No agent configuration detected (e.g., .claude or .gemini directory).")
   562→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "This is normal - some agents don't require a config directory.")
   563→		fmt.Fprintln(w)
   564→	}
   565→
   566→	// Build a set of detected agent names for pre-selection
   567→	detectedSet := make(map[agent.AgentName]struct{}, len(detected))
   568→	for _, ag := range detected {
   569→		detectedSet[ag.Name()] = struct{}{}
   570→	}
   571→
   572→	// Build options from registered agents
   573→	agentNames := agent.List()
   574→	options := make([]huh.Option[string], 0, len(agentNames))
   575→	for _, name := range agentNames {
   576→		ag, err := agent.Get(name)
   577→		if err != nil {
   578→			continue
   579→		}
   580→		// Only show agents that support hooks
   581→		if _, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport); !ok {
   582→			continue
   583→		}
   584→		label := string(ag.Type())
   585→		if name == agent.DefaultAgentName {
   586→			label += " (default)"
   587→		}
   588→		opt := huh.NewOption(label, string(name))
   589→		if _, isDetected := detectedSet[name]; isDetected {
   590→			opt = opt.Selected(true)
   591→		}
   592→		options = append(options, opt)
   593→	}
   594→
   595→	if len(options) == 0 {
   596→		return nil, errors.New("no agents with hook support available")
   597→	}
   598→
   599→	// Collect available agent names for the selector
   600→	availableNames := make([]string, 0, len(options))
   601→	for _, opt := range options {
   602→		availableNames = append(availableNames, opt.Value)
   603→	}
   604→
   605→	var selectedAgentNames []string
   606→	if selectFn != nil {
   607→		var err error
   608→		selectedAgentNames, err = selectFn(availableNames)
   609→		if err != nil {
   610→			return nil, err
   611→		}
   612→	} else {
   613→		form := NewAccessibleForm(
   614→			huh.NewGroup(
   615→				huh.NewMultiSelect[string]().
   616→					Title("Which agents are you using?").
   617→					Description("Use space to select, enter to confirm.").
   618→					Options(options...).
   619→					Value(&selectedAgentNames),
   620→			),
   621→		)
   622→		if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   623→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("agent selection cancelled: %w", err)
   624→		}
   625→	}
   626→
   627→	if len(selectedAgentNames) == 0 {
   628→		return nil, errors.New("no agents selected")
   629→	}
   630→
   631→	selectedAgents := make([]agent.Agent, 0, len(selectedAgentNames))
   632→	for _, name := range selectedAgentNames {
   633→		selectedAgent, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentName(name))
   634→		if err != nil {
   635→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get selected agent %s: %w", name, err)
   636→		}
   637→		selectedAgents = append(selectedAgents, selectedAgent)
   638→	}
   639→
   640→	agentTypes := make([]string, 0, len(selectedAgents))
   641→	for _, ag := range selectedAgents {
   642→		agentTypes = append(agentTypes, string(ag.Type()))
   643→	}
   644→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "\nSelected agents: %s\n\n", strings.Join(agentTypes, ", "))
   645→	return selectedAgents, nil
   646→}
   647→
   648→// canPromptInteractively checks if we can show interactive prompts.
   649→// Returns false when running in CI, tests, or other non-interactive environments.
   650→func canPromptInteractively() bool {
   651→	// Check for test environment
   652→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_TTY") != "" {
   653→		return os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_TTY") == "1"
   654→	}
   655→
   656→	// Check if /dev/tty is available
   657→	tty, err := os.OpenFile("/dev/tty", os.O_RDWR, 0)
   658→	if err != nil {
   659→		return false
   660→	}
   661→	_ = tty.Close()
   662→	return true
   663→}
   664→
   665→// printAgentError writes an error message followed by available agents and usage.
   666→func printAgentError(w io.Writer, message string) {
   667→	agents := agent.List()
   668→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "%s Available agents:\n", message)
   669→	fmt.Fprintln(w)
   670→	for _, a := range agents {
   671→		suffix := ""
   672→		if a == agent.DefaultAgentName {
   673→			suffix = "    (default)"
   674→		}
   675→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  %s%s\n", a, suffix)
   676→	}
   677→	fmt.Fprintln(w)
   678→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Usage: entire enable --agent <agent-name>")
   679→}
   680→
   681→// printMissingAgentError writes a helpful error listing available agents.
   682→func printMissingAgentError(w io.Writer) {
   683→	printAgentError(w, "Missing agent name.")
   684→}
   685→
   686→// printWrongAgentError writes a helpful error when an unknown agent name is provided.
   687→func printWrongAgentError(w io.Writer, name string) {
   688→	printAgentError(w, fmt.Sprintf("Unknown agent %q.", name))
   689→}
   690→
   691→// setupAgentHooksNonInteractive sets up hooks for a specific agent non-interactively.
   692→// If strategyName is provided, it sets the strategy; otherwise uses default.
   693→func setupAgentHooksNonInteractive(w io.Writer, ag agent.Agent, strategyName string, localDev, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry bool) error {
   694→	agentName := ag.Name()
   695→	// Check if agent supports hooks
   696→	hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
   697→	if !ok {
   698→		return fmt.Errorf("agent %s does not support hooks", agentName)
   699→	}
   700→
   701→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "Agent: %s\n\n", ag.Type())
   702→
   703→	// Install agent hooks (agent hooks don't depend on settings)
   704→	installedHooks, err := hookAgent.InstallHooks(localDev, forceHooks)
   705→	if err != nil {
   706→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install hooks for %s: %w", agentName, err)
   707→	}
   708→
   709→	// Setup .entire directory
   710→	if _, err := setupEntireDirectory(); err != nil {
   711→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup .entire directory: %w", err)
   712→	}
   713→
   714→	// Load existing settings to preserve other options (like strategy_options.push)
   715→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   716→	if err != nil {
   717→		// If we can't load, start with defaults
   718→		settings = &EntireSettings{Strategy: strategy.DefaultStrategyName}
   719→	}
   720→	settings.Enabled = true
   721→	if localDev {
   722→		settings.LocalDev = localDev
   723→	}
   724→
   725→	// Set push_sessions option if --skip-push-sessions flag was provided
   726→	if skipPushSessions {
   727→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   728→			settings.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]interface{})
   729→		}
   730→		settings.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"] = false
   731→	}
   732→
   733→	// Set strategy if provided
   734→	if strategyName != "" {
   735→		// Map display name to internal name if needed
   736→		internalStrategy := strategyName
   737→		if mapped, ok := strategyDisplayToInternal[strategyName]; ok {
   738→			internalStrategy = mapped
   739→		}
   740→		// Validate the strategy exists
   741→		if _, err := strategy.Get(internalStrategy); err != nil {
   742→			return fmt.Errorf("unknown strategy: %s (use manual-commit or auto-commit)", strategyName)
   743→		}
   744→		settings.Strategy = internalStrategy
   745→	}
   746→
   747→	// Handle telemetry for non-interactive mode
   748→	// Note: if telemetry is nil (not configured), it defaults to disabled
   749→	if !telemetry || os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT") != "" {
   750→		f := false
   751→		settings.Telemetry = &f
   752→	}
   753→
   754→	if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   755→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   756→	}
   757→
   758→	// Install git hooks AFTER saving settings (InstallGitHook reads local_dev from settings)
   759→	if _, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(true); err != nil {
   760→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hooks: %w", err)
   761→	}
   762→	strategy.CheckAndWarnHookManagers(w)
   763→
   764→	if installedHooks == 0 {
   765→		msg := fmt.Sprintf("Hooks for %s already installed", ag.Description())
   766→		if agentName == agent.AgentNameGemini {
   767→			msg += " (Preview)"
   768→		}
   769→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "%s\n", msg)
   770→	} else {
   771→		msg := fmt.Sprintf("Installed %d hooks for %s", installedHooks, ag.Description())
   772→		if agentName == agent.AgentNameGemini {
   773→			msg += " (Preview)"
   774→		}
   775→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "%s\n", msg)
   776→	}
   777→
   778→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Project configured (%s)\n", configDisplayProject)
   779→
   780→	// Let the strategy handle its own setup requirements (creates entire/checkpoints/v1 branch, etc.)
   781→	strat, err := strategy.Get(settings.Strategy)
   782→	if err != nil {
   783→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get strategy: %w", err)
   784→	}
   785→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   786→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup strategy: %w", err)
   787→	}
   788→
   789→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nReady.")
   790→
   791→	return nil
   792→}
   793→
   794→// validateSetupFlags checks that --local and --project flags are not both specified.
   795→func validateSetupFlags(useLocal, useProject bool) error {
   796→	if useLocal && useProject {
   797→		return errors.New("cannot specify both --project and --local")
   798→	}
   799→	return nil
   800→}
   801→
   802→// determineSettingsTarget decides whether to write to settings.local.json based on:
   803→// - Whether settings.json already exists
   804→// - The --local and --project flags
   805→// Returns (useLocal, showNotification).
   806→func determineSettingsTarget(entireDir string, useLocal, useProject bool) (bool, bool) {
   807→	// Explicit --local flag always uses local settings
   808→	if useLocal {
   809→		return true, false
   810→	}
   811→
   812→	// Explicit --project flag always uses project settings
   813→	if useProject {
   814→		return false, false
   815→	}
   816→
   817→	// No flags specified - check if settings file exists
   818→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(entireDir, paths.SettingsFileName)
   819→	if _, err := os.Stat(settingsPath); err == nil {
   820→		// Settings file exists - auto-redirect to local with notification
   821→		return true, true
   822→	}
   823→
   824→	// Settings file doesn't exist - create it
   825→	return false, false
   826→}
   827→
   828→// setupEntireDirectory creates the .entire directory and gitignore.
   829→// Returns true if the directory was created, false if it already existed.
   830→func setupEntireDirectory() (bool, error) { //nolint:unparam // already present in codebase
   831→	// Get absolute path for the .entire directory
   832→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
   833→	if err != nil {
   834→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir // Fallback to relative
   835→	}
   836→
   837→	// Check if directory already exists
   838→	created := false
   839→	if _, err := os.Stat(entireDirAbs); os.IsNotExist(err) {
   840→		created = true
   841→	}
   842→
   843→	// Create .entire directory
   844→	//nolint:gosec // G301: Project directory needs standard permissions for git
   845→	if err := os.MkdirAll(entireDirAbs, 0o755); err != nil {
   846→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to create .entire directory: %w", err)
   847→	}
   848→
   849→	// Create/update .gitignore with all required entries
   850→	if err := strategy.EnsureEntireGitignore(); err != nil {
   851→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to setup .gitignore: %w", err)
   852→	}
   853→
   854→	return created, nil
   855→}
   856→
   857→// setupGitHook installs the prepare-commit-msg hook for context trailers.
   858→func setupGitHook() error {
   859→	// Use shared implementation from strategy package
   860→	// The localDev setting is read from settings.json
   861→	_, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(false) // not silent - show output during setup
   862→	if err != nil {
   863→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hook: %w", err)
   864→	}
   865→	strategy.CheckAndWarnHookManagers(os.Stderr)
   866→	return nil
   867→}
   868→
   869→// newSetupGitHookCmd creates the standalone git-hook setup command
   870→func newSetupGitHookCmd() *cobra.Command {
   871→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
   872→		Use:    "git-hook",
   873→		Short:  "Install git hook for session context trailers",
   874→		Hidden: true, // Hidden as it's mainly for testing
   875→		RunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   876→			return setupGitHook()
   877→		},
   878→	}
   879→
   880→	return cmd
   881→}
   882→
   883→func newCurlBashPostInstallCmd() *cobra.Command {
   884→	return &cobra.Command{
   885→		Use:    "curl-bash-post-install",
   886→		Short:  "Post-install tasks for curl|bash installer",
   887→		Hidden: true,
   888→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   889→			w := cmd.OutOrStdout()
   890→			if err := promptShellCompletion(w); err != nil {
   891→				fmt.Fprintf(w, "Note: Shell completion setup skipped: %v\n", err)
   892→			}
   893→			return nil
   894→		},
   895→	}
   896→}
   897→
   898→// shellCompletionComment is the comment preceding the completion line
   899→const shellCompletionComment = "# Entire CLI shell completion"
   900→
   901→// errUnsupportedShell is returned when the user's shell is not supported for completion.
   902→var errUnsupportedShell = errors.New("unsupported shell")
   903→
   904→// shellCompletionTarget returns the rc file path and completion lines for the
   905→// user's current shell.
   906→func shellCompletionTarget() (shellName, rcFile, completionLine string, err error) {
   907→	home, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   908→	if err != nil {
   909→		return "", "", "", fmt.Errorf("cannot determine home directory: %w", err)
   910→	}
   911→
   912→	shell := os.Getenv("SHELL")
   913→	switch {
   914→	case strings.Contains(shell, "zsh"):
   915→		return "Zsh",
   916→			filepath.Join(home, ".zshrc"),
   917→			"autoload -Uz compinit && compinit && source <(entire completion zsh)",
   918→			nil
   919→	case strings.Contains(shell, "bash"):
   920→		bashRC := filepath.Join(home, ".bashrc")
   921→		if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(home, ".bash_profile")); err == nil {
   922→			bashRC = filepath.Join(home, ".bash_profile")
   923→		}
   924→		return "Bash",
   925→			bashRC,
   926→			"source <(entire completion bash)",
   927→			nil
   928→	case strings.Contains(shell, "fish"):
   929→		return "Fish",
   930→			filepath.Join(home, ".config", "fish", "config.fish"),
   931→			"entire completion fish | source",
   932→			nil
   933→	default:
   934→		return "", "", "", errUnsupportedShell
   935→	}
   936→}
   937→
   938→// promptShellCompletion offers to add shell completion to the user's rc file.
   939→// Only prompts if completion is not already configured.
   940→func promptShellCompletion(w io.Writer) error {
   941→	shellName, rcFile, completionLine, err := shellCompletionTarget()
   942→	if err != nil {
   943→		if errors.Is(err, errUnsupportedShell) {
   944→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "Note: Shell completion not available for your shell. Supported: zsh, bash, fish.\n")
   945→			return nil
   946→		}
   947→		return fmt.Errorf("shell completion: %w", err)
   948→	}
   949→
   950→	if isCompletionConfigured(rcFile) {
   951→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Shell completion already configured in %s\n", rcFile)
   952→		return nil
   953→	}
   954→
   955→	var selected string
   956→	form := NewAccessibleForm(
   957→		huh.NewGroup(
   958→			huh.NewSelect[string]().
   959→				Title(fmt.Sprintf("Enable shell completion? (detected: %s)", shellName)).
   960→				Options(
   961→					huh.NewOption("Yes", "yes"),
   962→					huh.NewOption("No", "no"),
   963→				).
   964→				Value(&selected),
   965→		),
   966→	)
   967→
   968→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   969→		//nolint:nilerr // User cancelled - not a fatal error, just skip
   970→		return nil
   971→	}
   972→
   973→	if selected != "yes" {
   974→		return nil
   975→	}
   976→
   977→	if err := appendShellCompletion(rcFile, completionLine); err != nil {
   978→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to update %s: %w", rcFile, err)
   979→	}
   980→
   981→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Shell completion added to %s\n", rcFile)
   982→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Restart your shell to activate")
   983→
   984→	return nil
   985→}
   986→
   987→// isCompletionConfigured checks if shell completion is already in the rc file.
   988→func isCompletionConfigured(rcFile string) bool {
   989→	//nolint:gosec // G304: rcFile is constructed from home dir + known filename, not user input
   990→	content, err := os.ReadFile(rcFile)
   991→	if err != nil {
   992→		return false // File doesn't exist or can't read, treat as not configured
   993→	}
   994→	return strings.Contains(string(content), "entire completion")
   995→}
   996→
   997→// appendShellCompletion adds the completion line to the rc file.
   998→func appendShellCompletion(rcFile, completionLine string) error {
   999→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(rcFile), 0o700); err != nil {
  1000→		return fmt.Errorf("creating directory: %w", err)
  1001→	}
  1002→	//nolint:gosec // G302: Shell rc files need 0644 for user readability
  1003→	f, err := os.OpenFile(rcFile, os.O_APPEND|os.O_CREATE|os.O_WRONLY, 0o644)
  1004→	if err != nil {
  1005→		return fmt.Errorf("opening file: %w", err)
  1006→	}
  1007→	defer f.Close()
  1008→
  1009→	_, err = f.WriteString("\n" + shellCompletionComment + "\n" + completionLine + "\n")
  1010→	if err != nil {
  1011→		return fmt.Errorf("writing completion: %w", err)
  1012→	}
  1013→	return nil
  1014→}
  1015→
  1016→// promptTelemetryConsent asks the user if they want to enable telemetry.
  1017→// It modifies settings.Telemetry based on the user's choice or flags.
  1018→// The caller is responsible for saving settings.
  1019→func promptTelemetryConsent(settings *EntireSettings, telemetryFlag bool) error {
  1020→	// Handle --telemetry=false flag first (always overrides existing setting)
  1021→	if !telemetryFlag {
  1022→		f := false
  1023→		settings.Telemetry = &f
  1024→		return nil
  1025→	}
  1026→
  1027→	// Skip if already asked
  1028→	if settings.Telemetry != nil {
  1029→		return nil
  1030→	}
  1031→
  1032→	// Skip if env var disables telemetry (record as disabled)
  1033→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT") != "" {
  1034→		f := false
  1035→		settings.Telemetry = &f
  1036→		return nil
  1037→	}
  1038→
  1039→	consent := true // Default to Yes
  1040→	form := NewAccessibleForm(
  1041→		huh.NewGroup(
  1042→			huh.NewConfirm().
  1043→				Title("Help improve Entire CLI?").
  1044→				Description("Share anonymous usage data. No code or personal info collected.").
  1045→				Affirmative("Yes").
  1046→				Negative("No").
  1047→				Value(&consent),
  1048→		),
  1049→	)
  1050→
  1051→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
  1052→		return fmt.Errorf("telemetry prompt: %w", err)
  1053→	}
  1054→
  1055→	settings.Telemetry = &consent
  1056→	return nil
  1057→}
  1058→
  1059→// runUninstall completely removes Entire from the repository.
  1060→func runUninstall(w, errW io.Writer, force bool) error {
  1061→	// Check if we're in a git repository
  1062→	if _, err := paths.RepoRoot(); err != nil {
  1063→		fmt.Fprintln(errW, "Not a git repository. Nothing to uninstall.")
  1064→		return NewSilentError(errors.New("not a git repository"))
  1065→	}
  1066→
  1067→	// Gather counts for display
  1068→	sessionStateCount := countSessionStates()
  1069→	shadowBranchCount := countShadowBranches()
  1070→	gitHooksInstalled := strategy.IsGitHookInstalled()
  1071→	claudeHooksInstalled := checkClaudeCodeHooksInstalled()
  1072→	geminiHooksInstalled := checkGeminiCLIHooksInstalled()
  1073→	entireDirExists := checkEntireDirExists()
  1074→
  1075→	// Check if there's anything to uninstall
  1076→	if !entireDirExists && !gitHooksInstalled && sessionStateCount == 0 &&
  1077→		shadowBranchCount == 0 && !claudeHooksInstalled && !geminiHooksInstalled {
  1078→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "Entire is not installed in this repository.")
  1079→		return nil
  1080→	}
  1081→
  1082→	// Show confirmation prompt unless --force
  1083→	if !force {
  1084→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nThis will completely remove Entire from this repository:")
  1085→		if entireDirExists {
  1086→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - .entire/ directory")
  1087→		}
  1088→		if gitHooksInstalled {
  1089→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Git hooks (prepare-commit-msg, commit-msg, post-commit, pre-push)")
  1090→		}
  1091→		if sessionStateCount > 0 {
  1092→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "  - Session state files (%d)\n", sessionStateCount)
  1093→		}
  1094→		if shadowBranchCount > 0 {
  1095→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "  - Shadow branches (%d)\n", shadowBranchCount)
  1096→		}
  1097→		switch {
  1098→		case claudeHooksInstalled && geminiHooksInstalled:
  1099→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Agent hooks (Claude Code, Gemini CLI)")
  1100→		case claudeHooksInstalled:
  1101→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Agent hooks (Claude Code)")
  1102→		case geminiHooksInstalled:
  1103→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Agent hooks (Gemini CLI)")
  1104→		}
  1105→		fmt.Fprintln(w)
  1106→
  1107→		var confirmed bool
  1108→		form := NewAccessibleForm(
  1109→			huh.NewGroup(
  1110→				huh.NewConfirm().
  1111→					Title("Are you sure you want to uninstall Entire?").
  1112→					Affirmative("Yes, uninstall").
  1113→					Negative("Cancel").
  1114→					Value(&confirmed),
  1115→			),
  1116→		)
  1117→
  1118→		if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
  1119→			return fmt.Errorf("confirmation cancelled: %w", err)
  1120→		}
  1121→
  1122→		if !confirmed {
  1123→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "Uninstall cancelled.")
  1124→			return nil
  1125→		}
  1126→	}
  1127→
  1128→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nUninstalling Entire CLI...")
  1129→
  1130→	// 1. Remove agent hooks (lowest risk)
  1131→	if err := removeAgentHooks(w); err != nil {
  1132→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove agent hooks: %v\n", err)
  1133→	}
  1134→
  1135→	// 2. Remove git hooks
  1136→	removed, err := strategy.RemoveGitHook()
  1137→	if err != nil {
  1138→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove git hooks: %v\n", err)
  1139→	} else if removed > 0 {
  1140→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Removed git hooks (%d)\n", removed)
  1141→	}
  1142→
  1143→	// 3. Remove session state files
  1144→	statesRemoved, err := removeAllSessionStates()
  1145→	if err != nil {
  1146→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove session states: %v\n", err)
  1147→	} else if statesRemoved > 0 {
  1148→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Removed session states (%d)\n", statesRemoved)
  1149→	}
  1150→
  1151→	// 4. Remove .entire/ directory
  1152→	if err := removeEntireDirectory(); err != nil {
  1153→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove .entire directory: %v\n", err)
  1154→	} else if entireDirExists {
  1155→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Removed .entire directory")
  1156→	}
  1157→
  1158→	// 5. Remove shadow branches
  1159→	branchesRemoved, err := removeAllShadowBranches()
  1160→	if err != nil {
  1161→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove shadow branches: %v\n", err)
  1162→	} else if branchesRemoved > 0 {
  1163→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Removed %d shadow branches\n", branchesRemoved)
  1164→	}
  1165→
  1166→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nEntire CLI uninstalled successfully.")
  1167→	return nil
  1168→}
  1169→
  1170→// countSessionStates returns the number of active session state files.
  1171→func countSessionStates() int {
  1172→	store, err := session.NewStateStore()
  1173→	if err != nil {
  1174→		return 0
  1175→	}
  1176→	states, err := store.List(context.Background())
  1177→	if err != nil {
  1178→		return 0
  1179→	}
  1180→	return len(states)
  1181→}
  1182→
  1183→// countShadowBranches returns the number of shadow branches.
  1184→func countShadowBranches() int {
  1185→	branches, err := strategy.ListShadowBranches()
  1186→	if err != nil {
  1187→		return 0
  1188→	}
  1189→	return len(branches)
  1190→}
  1191→
  1192→// checkClaudeCodeHooksInstalled checks if Claude Code hooks are installed.
  1193→func checkClaudeCodeHooksInstalled() bool {
  1194→	ag, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameClaudeCode)
  1195→	if err != nil {
  1196→		return false
  1197→	}
  1198→	hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
  1199→	if !ok {
  1200→		return false
  1201→	}
  1202→	return hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()
  1203→}
  1204→
  1205→// checkGeminiCLIHooksInstalled checks if Gemini CLI hooks are installed.
  1206→func checkGeminiCLIHooksInstalled() bool {
  1207→	ag, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameGemini)
  1208→	if err != nil {
  1209→		return false
  1210→	}
  1211→	hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
  1212→	if !ok {
  1213→		return false
  1214→	}
  1215→	return hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()
  1216→}
  1217→
  1218→// checkEntireDirExists checks if the .entire directory exists.
  1219→func checkEntireDirExists() bool {
  1220→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
  1221→	if err != nil {
  1222→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir
  1223→	}
  1224→	_, err = os.Stat(entireDirAbs)
  1225→	return err == nil
  1226→}
  1227→
  1228→// removeAgentHooks removes hooks from all agents that support hooks.
  1229→func removeAgentHooks(w io.Writer) error {
  1230→	var errs []error
  1231→
  1232→	// Remove Claude Code hooks
  1233→	claudeAgent, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameClaudeCode)
  1234→	if err == nil {
  1235→		if hookAgent, ok := claudeAgent.(agent.HookSupport); ok {
  1236→			wasInstalled := hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()
  1237→			if err := hookAgent.UninstallHooks(); err != nil {
  1238→				errs = append(errs, err)
  1239→			} else if wasInstalled {
  1240→				fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Removed Claude Code hooks")
  1241→			}
  1242→		}
  1243→	}
  1244→
  1245→	// Remove Gemini CLI hooks
  1246→	geminiAgent, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameGemini)
  1247→	if err == nil {
  1248→		if hookAgent, ok := geminiAgent.(agent.HookSupport); ok {
  1249→			wasInstalled := hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()
  1250→			if err := hookAgent.UninstallHooks(); err != nil {
  1251→				errs = append(errs, err)
  1252→			} else if wasInstalled {
  1253→				fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Removed Gemini CLI hooks")
  1254→			}
  1255→		}
  1256→	}
  1257→
  1258→	return errors.Join(errs...)
  1259→}
  1260→
  1261→// removeAllSessionStates removes all session state files and the directory.
  1262→func removeAllSessionStates() (int, error) {
  1263→	store, err := session.NewStateStore()
  1264→	if err != nil {
  1265→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create state store: %w", err)
  1266→	}
  1267→
  1268→	// Count states before removing
  1269→	states, err := store.List(context.Background())
  1270→	if err != nil {
  1271→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to list session states: %w", err)
  1272→	}
  1273→	count := len(states)
  1274→
  1275→	// Remove the entire directory
  1276→	if err := store.RemoveAll(); err != nil {
  1277→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to remove session states: %w", err)
  1278→	}
  1279→
  1280→	return count, nil
  1281→}
  1282→
  1283→// removeEntireDirectory removes the .entire directory.
  1284→func removeEntireDirectory() error {
  1285→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
  1286→	if err != nil {
  1287→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir
  1288→	}
  1289→	if err := os.RemoveAll(entireDirAbs); err != nil {
  1290→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove .entire directory: %w", err)
  1291→	}
  1292→	return nil
  1293→}
  1294→
  1295→// removeAllShadowBranches removes all shadow branches.
  1296→func removeAllShadowBranches() (int, error) {
  1297→	branches, err := strategy.ListShadowBranches()
  1298→	if err != nil {
