   100→
   101→// GetAgentsWithHooksInstalled returns names of agents that have hooks installed.
   102→func GetAgentsWithHooksInstalled() []agent.AgentName {
   103→	var installed []agent.AgentName
   104→	for _, name := range agent.List() {
   105→		ag, err := agent.Get(name)
   106→		if err != nil {
   107→			continue
   108→		}
   109→		if hs, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport); ok && hs.AreHooksInstalled() {
   110→			installed = append(installed, name)
   111→		}
   112→	}
   113→	return installed
   114→}
   115→
   116→// JoinAgentNames joins agent names into a comma-separated string.
   117→func JoinAgentNames(names []agent.AgentName) string {
   118→	strs := make([]string, len(names))
   119→	for i, n := range names {
   120→		strs[i] = string(n)
   121→	}
   122→	return strings.Join(strs, ",")
   123→}

     1→package claudecode
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"encoding/json"
     5→	"fmt"
     6→	"os"

    10→
    11→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"

    14→)
    15→
    16→// Ensure ClaudeCodeAgent implements HookSupport and HookHandler
    17→var (
    18→	_ agent.HookSupport = (*ClaudeCodeAgent)(nil)
    19→	_ agent.HookHandler = (*ClaudeCodeAgent)(nil)
    20→)
    21→
    22→// Claude Code hook names - these become subcommands under `entire hooks claude-code`
    23→const (
    24→	HookNameSessionStart     = "session-start"
    25→	HookNameSessionEnd       = "session-end"
    26→	HookNameStop             = "stop"
    27→	HookNameUserPromptSubmit = "user-prompt-submit"
    28→	HookNamePreTask          = "pre-task"
    29→	HookNamePostTask         = "post-task"
    30→	HookNamePostTodo         = "post-todo"
    31→)
    32→
    33→// ClaudeSettingsFileName is the settings file used by Claude Code.
    34→// This is Claude-specific and not shared with other agents.
    35→const ClaudeSettingsFileName = "settings.json"
    36→
    37→// metadataDenyRule blocks Claude from reading Entire session metadata
    38→const metadataDenyRule = "Read(./.entire/metadata/**)"
    39→
    40→// GetHookNames returns the hook verbs Claude Code supports.
    41→// These become subcommands: entire hooks claude-code <verb>
    42→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetHookNames() []string {
    43→	return []string{
    44→		HookNameSessionStart,
    45→		HookNameSessionEnd,
    46→		HookNameStop,
    47→		HookNameUserPromptSubmit,
    48→		HookNamePreTask,
    49→		HookNamePostTask,
    50→		HookNamePostTodo,
    51→	}
    52→}
    53→
    54→// entireHookPrefixes are command prefixes that identify Entire hooks (both old and new formats)
    55→var entireHookPrefixes = []string{
    56→	"entire ",
    57→	"go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go ",
    58→}
    59→
    60→// InstallHooks installs Claude Code hooks in .claude/settings.json.
    61→// If force is true, removes existing Entire hooks before installing.
    62→// Returns the number of hooks installed.
    63→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error) {
    64→	// Use repo root instead of CWD to find .claude directory
    65→	// This ensures hooks are installed correctly when run from a subdirectory
    66→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    67→	if err != nil {
    68→		// Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo (e.g., during tests)
    69→		repoRoot, err = os.Getwd() //nolint:forbidigo // Intentional fallback when RepoRoot() fails (tests run outside git repos)
    70→		if err != nil {
    71→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to get current directory: %w", err)
    72→		}
    73→	}
    74→
    75→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".claude", ClaudeSettingsFileName)
    76→
    77→	// Read existing settings if they exist
    78→	var rawSettings map[string]json.RawMessage
    79→
    80→	// rawHooks preserves unknown hook types (e.g., "Notification", "SubagentStop")
    81→	var rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage
    82→
    83→	// rawPermissions preserves unknown permission fields (e.g., "ask")
    84→	var rawPermissions map[string]json.RawMessage
    85→
    86→	existingData, readErr := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from cwd + fixed path
    87→	if readErr == nil {
    88→		if err := json.Unmarshal(existingData, &rawSettings); err != nil {
    89→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse existing settings.json: %w", err)
    90→		}
    91→		if hooksRaw, ok := rawSettings["hooks"]; ok {
    92→			if err := json.Unmarshal(hooksRaw, &rawHooks); err != nil {
    93→				return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hooks in settings.json: %w", err)
    94→			}
    95→		}
    96→		if permRaw, ok := rawSettings["permissions"]; ok {
    97→			if err := json.Unmarshal(permRaw, &rawPermissions); err != nil {
    98→				return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse permissions in settings.json: %w", err)
    99→			}
   100→		}

```
     1→# Agent Implementation Guide
     2→
     3→## Architecture Overview
     4→
     5→The Entire CLI uses **inversion of control** for agent integration: agents are passive data providers that translate their native hook payloads into normalized lifecycle events, and the framework handles all orchestration (state transitions, file detection, checkpoint saving, metadata generation). The flow is:
     6→
     7→```
     8→Agent hook invocation → ParseHookEvent() → Event → DispatchLifecycleEvent() → framework actions
     9→```
    10→
    11→An agent never calls strategy methods or manages session state directly. It only answers questions: "What event just happened?" and "What does the transcript say?"
    12→
    13→## Quick Reference
    14→
    15→### Core Interface (`Agent`)
    16→
    17→Every agent must implement all 19 methods on the `Agent` interface:
    18→
    19→| Group | Method | Purpose |
    20→|-------|--------|---------|
    21→| **Identity** | `Name()` | Registry key (e.g., `"claude-code"`) |
    22→| | `Type()` | Display name for metadata (e.g., `"Claude Code"`) |
    23→| | `Description()` | Human-readable description for UI |
    24→| | `DetectPresence()` | Check if agent is configured in the repo |
    25→| | `ProtectedDirs()` | Directories to preserve during rewind |
    26→| **Event Mapping** | `HookNames()` | Hook verbs that become CLI subcommands |
    27→| | `ParseHookEvent()` | **Core contribution surface** - translate native hooks to Events |
    28→| **Transcript** | `ReadTranscript()` | Read raw transcript bytes |
    29→| | `ChunkTranscript()` | Split large transcripts at format-aware boundaries |
    30→| | `ReassembleTranscript()` | Recombine chunks into a single transcript |
    31→| **Session Management** | `GetHookConfigPath()` | Path to hook config file |
    32→| | `SupportsHooks()` | Whether agent supports lifecycle hooks |
    33→| | `ParseHookInput()` | Parse hook callback input from stdin |
    34→| | `GetSessionID()` | Extract session ID from hook input |
    35→| | `GetSessionDir()` | Where agent stores session data |
    36→| | `ResolveSessionFile()` | Path to session transcript file |
    37→| | `ReadSession()` | Read session data from agent's storage |
    38→| | `WriteSession()` | Write session data for resumption |
    39→| | `FormatResumeCommand()` | Command to resume a session |
    40→
    41→### Optional Interfaces
    42→
    43→| Interface | Methods | When to implement |
    44→|-----------|---------|-------------------|
    45→| `HookSupport` | `InstallHooks`, `UninstallHooks`, `AreHooksInstalled`, `GetSupportedHooks` | Agent uses a config file for hook registration (e.g., `settings.json`) |
    46→| `HookHandler` | `GetHookNames` | **Required for CLI hook registration** — `entire hooks <agent> <verb>` subcommands are only created for agents implementing this interface. Typically delegates to `HookNames()`. |
    47→| `TranscriptAnalyzer` | `GetTranscriptPosition`, `ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset`, `ExtractPrompts`, `ExtractSummary` | You want richer checkpoints with transcript-derived file lists and prompts |
    48→| `TranscriptPreparer` | `PrepareTranscript` | Agent writes transcripts asynchronously and needs a flush/sync step |
    49→| `TokenCalculator` | `CalculateTokenUsage` | Agent's transcript contains token usage data |
    50→| `SubagentAwareExtractor` | `ExtractAllModifiedFiles`, `CalculateTotalTokenUsage` | Agent spawns subagents (like Claude Code's Task tool) |
    51→| `FileWatcher` | `GetWatchPaths`, `OnFileChange` | Agent doesn't support hooks; uses file-based detection instead |
    52→
    53→## Step-by-Step Implementation Guide
    54→
    55→### Step 1: Create Package
    56→
    57→Create a new directory under `cmd/entire/cli/agent/`:
    58→
    59→```
    60→cmd/entire/cli/agent/youragent/
    61→├── youragent.go          # Core Agent implementation + init()
    62→├── lifecycle.go          # ParseHookEvent + compile-time assertions
    63→├── types.go              # Hook input structs, transcript types, tool constants
    64→├── hooks.go              # HookSupport implementation (if applicable)
    65→├── transcript.go         # TranscriptAnalyzer implementation (if applicable)
    66→├── lifecycle_test.go     # Tests for ParseHookEvent
    67→├── hooks_test.go         # Tests for hook installation
    68→└── transcript_test.go    # Tests for transcript analysis
    69→```
    70→
    71→### Step 2: Define Types (`types.go`)
    72→
    73→Define structs matching your agent's native hook JSON payloads:
    74→
    75→```go
    76→package youragent
    77→
    78→// Settings file structure (for HookSupport)
    79→type YourAgentSettings struct {
    80→    Hooks YourAgentHooks `json:"hooks"`
    81→}
    82→
    83→type YourAgentHooks struct {
    84→    SessionStart []HookMatcher `json:"SessionStart,omitempty"`
    85→    SessionEnd   []HookMatcher `json:"SessionEnd,omitempty"`
    86→    // ... other hook types your agent supports
    87→}
    88→
    89→type HookMatcher struct {
    90→    Matcher string      `json:"matcher,omitempty"`
    91→    Hooks   []HookEntry `json:"hooks"`
    92→}
    93→
    94→type HookEntry struct {
    95→    Type    string `json:"type"`
    96→    Command string `json:"command"`
    97→}
    98→
    99→// Hook input structs - match your agent's JSON payloads
   100→
   101→type sessionInfoRaw struct {
   102→    SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
   103→    TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
   104→}
   105→
   106→type promptInputRaw struct {
   107→    SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
   108→    TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
   109→    Prompt         string `json:"prompt"`
   110→}
   111→
   112→// Tool constants - tools in your agent that modify files
   113→const (
   114→    ToolWrite = "write_file"
   115→    ToolEdit  = "edit_file"
   116→)
   117→
   118→var FileModificationTools = []string{ToolWrite, ToolEdit}
   119→```
   120→
   121→### Step 3: Implement Core Agent Interface (`youragent.go`)
   122→
   123→```go
   124→package youragent
   125→
   126→import (
   127→    "errors"
   128→    "fmt"
   129→    "io"
   130→    "os"
   131→    "path/filepath"
   132→
   133→    "github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
   134→    "github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
   135→)
   136→
   137→//nolint:gochecknoinits // Agent self-registration is the intended pattern
   138→func init() {
   139→    agent.Register("your-agent", NewYourAgent)
   140→}
   141→
   142→type YourAgent struct{}
   143→
   144→func NewYourAgent() agent.Agent {
   145→    return &YourAgent{}
   146→}
   147→
   148→// --- Identity ---
   149→
   150→func (a *YourAgent) Name() agent.AgentName    { return "your-agent" }
   151→func (a *YourAgent) Type() agent.AgentType     { return "Your Agent" }
   152→func (a *YourAgent) Description() string       { return "Your Agent - description here" }
   153→func (a *YourAgent) ProtectedDirs() []string   { return []string{".youragent"} }
   154→
   155→func (a *YourAgent) DetectPresence() (bool, error) {
   156→    repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   157→    if err != nil {
   158→        repoRoot = "."
   159→    }
   160→    _, err = os.Stat(filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".youragent"))
   161→    if err == nil {
   162→        return true, nil
   163→    }
   164→    return false, nil
   165→}
   166→
   167→// --- Transcript Storage ---
   168→
   169→func (a *YourAgent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {
   170→    data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef)
   171→    if err != nil {
   172→        return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   173→    }
   174→    return data, nil
   175→}
   176→
   177→func (a *YourAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
   178→    // Use JSONL chunking for line-based formats
   179→    return agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   180→    // Or implement format-specific chunking (see geminicli for JSON example)
   181→}
   182→
   183→func (a *YourAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
   184→    return agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks), nil
   185→}
   186→
   187→// --- Session Management ---
   188→
   189→func (a *YourAgent) GetHookConfigPath() string                     { return ".youragent/settings.json" }
   190→func (a *YourAgent) SupportsHooks() bool                           { return true }
   191→func (a *YourAgent) GetSessionID(input *agent.HookInput) string    { return input.SessionID }
   192→func (a *YourAgent) FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string   { return "youragent --resume " + sessionID }
   193→
   194→// ParseHookInput is part of the Agent interface and is called by integration tests.
   195→// Provide a real implementation that populates at least SessionID and SessionRef.
   196→func (a *YourAgent) ParseHookInput(hookType agent.HookType, r io.Reader) (*agent.HookInput, error) {
   197→    raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](r)
   198→    if err != nil {
   199→        return nil, err
   200→    }
   201→    return &agent.HookInput{
   202→        SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   203→        SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   204→    }, nil
   205→}
   206→
   207→func (a *YourAgent) GetSessionDir(_ string) (string, error) {
   208→    return "", errors.New("not implemented")
   209→}
   210→
   211→func (a *YourAgent) ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string {
   212→    return filepath.Join(sessionDir, agentSessionID+".jsonl")
   213→}
   214→
   215→func (a *YourAgent) ReadSession(_ *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   216→    return nil, errors.New("not implemented")
   217→}
   218→
   219→func (a *YourAgent) WriteSession(_ *agent.AgentSession) error {
   220→    return errors.New("not implemented")
   221→}
   222→```
   223→
   224→### Step 4: Implement `ParseHookEvent` (`lifecycle.go`)
   225→
   226→This is the **main contribution surface** for new agents. Map each of your agent's native hook names to the normalized `EventType`:
   227→
   228→```go
   229→package youragent
   230→
   231→import (
   232→    "io"
   233→    "time"
   234→
   235→    "github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
   236→)
   237→
   238→// Hook name constants - these become CLI subcommands
   239→const (
   240→    HookNameSessionStart = "session-start"
   241→    HookNameSessionEnd   = "session-end"
   242→    HookNamePromptSubmit = "prompt-submit"
   243→    HookNameResponse     = "response"
   244→)
   245→
   246→func (a *YourAgent) HookNames() []string {
   247→    return []string{
   248→        HookNameSessionStart,
   249→        HookNameSessionEnd,
   250→        HookNamePromptSubmit,
   251→        HookNameResponse,
   252→    }
   253→}
   254→
   255→func (a *YourAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   256→    switch hookName {
   257→    case HookNameSessionStart:
   258→        raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   259→        if err != nil {
   260→            return nil, err
   261→        }
   262→        return &agent.Event{
   263→            Type:       agent.SessionStart,
   264→            SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   265→            SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   266→            Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   267→        }, nil
   268→
   269→    case HookNamePromptSubmit:
   270→        raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[promptInputRaw](stdin)
   271→        if err != nil {
   272→            return nil, err
   273→        }
   274→        return &agent.Event{
   275→            Type:       agent.TurnStart,
   276→            SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   277→            SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   278→            Prompt:     raw.Prompt,
   279→            Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   280→        }, nil
   281→
   282→    case HookNameResponse:
   283→        raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   284→        if err != nil {
   285→            return nil, err
   286→        }
   287→        return &agent.Event{
   288→            Type:       agent.TurnEnd,
   289→            SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   290→            SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   291→            Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   292→        }, nil
   293→
   294→    case HookNameSessionEnd:
   295→        raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   296→        if err != nil {
   297→            return nil, err
   298→        }
   299→        return &agent.Event{
   300→            Type:       agent.SessionEnd,
   301→            SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   302→            SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   303→            Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   304→        }, nil
   305→
   306→    default:
   307→        // Unknown hooks have no lifecycle significance
   308→        return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil
   309→    }
   310→}
   311→```
   312→
   313→Key decisions in `ParseHookEvent`:
   314→
   315→- **Return `nil, nil`** for hooks with no lifecycle significance (pass-through hooks). This is not an error - it tells the framework to do nothing.
   316→- **Every event must include the required fields for its `EventType`** (see the [Event Field Requirements](#event-field-requirements) table). `SessionID` is always required; `SessionRef` is required for most event types but optional for `Compaction` and `SessionEnd`.
   317→- **`TurnStart` should include `Prompt`** if available - it's used for commit message generation.
   318→- **Use `agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[T]`** - the generic helper reads stdin and unmarshals JSON in one step.
   319→- **Set `Timestamp` to `time.Now()`** - the framework uses this for ordering.
   320→
   321→### Step 5: Choose and Implement Optional Interfaces
   322→
   323→See the [Optional Interface Decision Tree](#optional-interface-decision-tree) section below.
   324→
   325→### Step 6: Register via `init()`
   326→
   327→Registration happens in `init()` in your main agent file. The import side-effect pattern ensures your agent is available when the CLI starts:
   328→
   329→```go
   330→//nolint:gochecknoinits // Agent self-registration is the intended pattern
   331→func init() {
   332→    agent.Register("your-agent", NewYourAgent)
   333→}
   334→```
   335→
   336→Then add a blank import in the CLI's command setup to ensure the package is loaded:
   337→
   338→```go
   339→// In cmd/entire/cli/commands/ or wherever agents are imported
   340→import (
   341→    _ "github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/youragent"
   342→)
   343→```
   344→
   345→### Step 7: Add Compile-Time Interface Assertions
   346→
   347→In `lifecycle.go` (or whichever file implements the optional interfaces), add compile-time checks:
   348→
   349→```go
   350→// Compile-time interface assertions
   351→var (
   352→    _ agent.TranscriptAnalyzer = (*YourAgent)(nil)
   353→    _ agent.TokenCalculator    = (*YourAgent)(nil)
   354→    // Add one line per optional interface you implement
   355→)
   356→```
   357→
   358→Also add assertions for `HookSupport` and `HookHandler` in `hooks.go` if applicable:
   359→
   360→```go
   361→var (
   362→    _ agent.HookSupport = (*YourAgent)(nil)
   363→    _ agent.HookHandler = (*YourAgent)(nil)
   364→)
   365→```
   366→
   367→### Step 8: Implement Hook Installation (if `HookSupport`)
   368→
   369→If your agent uses a JSON config file for hooks (like Claude Code's `.claude/settings.json` or Gemini's `.gemini/settings.json`), implement `HookSupport`:
   370→
   371→```go
   372→func (a *YourAgent) InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error) {
   373→    // 1. Find repo root
   374→    repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   375→    if err != nil {
   376→        return 0, err
   377→    }
   378→
   379→    // 2. Read existing settings (preserve unknown fields)
   380→    settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".youragent", "settings.json")
   381→    // ... read and parse ...
   382→
   383→    // 3. Build hook commands
   384→    // localDev mode uses an agent-specific env var (e.g., ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR},
   385→    // ${GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR}) that the agent expands at runtime. Choose a name
   386→    // for your agent and add it to entireHookPrefixes so existing hooks can be
   387→    // detected/removed during install/uninstall.
   388→    var cmdPrefix string
   389→    if localDev {
   390→        cmdPrefix = "go run ${YOUR_AGENT_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks your-agent "
   391→    } else {
   392→        cmdPrefix = "entire hooks your-agent "
   393→    }
   394→
   395→    // 4. Add hooks if they don't exist (idempotent)
   396→    // 5. Write settings back (preserving unknown fields)
   397→
   398→    return count, nil
   399→}
   400→
   401→func (a *YourAgent) UninstallHooks() error         { /* reverse of install */ }
   402→func (a *YourAgent) AreHooksInstalled() bool        { /* check settings file */ }
   403→func (a *YourAgent) GetSupportedHooks() []agent.HookType { /* list supported types */ }
   404→```
   405→
   406→Also implement `HookHandler` — this is required for the CLI to register `entire hooks <agent> <verb>` subcommands:
   407→
   408→```go
   409→func (a *YourAgent) GetHookNames() []string {
   410→    return a.HookNames() // delegate to the core interface method
   411→}
   412→```
   413→
   414→### Step 9: Write Tests
   415→
   416→Test `ParseHookEvent` for every hook name your agent supports. See [Testing Patterns](#testing-patterns) below.
   417→
   418→## Event Mapping Reference
   419→
   420→The framework dispatcher (`DispatchLifecycleEvent` in `lifecycle.go`) handles each event type as follows:
   421→
   422→| Event Type | Framework Actions | Claude Code Hook | Gemini CLI Hook |
   423→|------------|-------------------|------------------|-----------------|
   424→| `SessionStart` | Shows banner, checks concurrent sessions, fires state machine transition | `session-start` | `session-start` |
   425→| `TurnStart` | Captures pre-prompt state (git status, transcript position), ensures strategy setup, initializes session | `user-prompt-submit` | `before-agent` |
   426→| `TurnEnd` | Validates transcript, extracts metadata (prompts, summary, files), detects file changes via git status, saves step + checkpoint, transitions phase to IDLE | `stop` | `after-agent` |
   427→| `Compaction` | Fires compaction transition (stays ACTIVE), resets transcript offset | *(not used)* | `pre-compress` |
   428→| `SessionEnd` | Marks session as ENDED in state machine | `session-end` | `session-end` |
   429→| `SubagentStart` | Captures pre-task state (git status snapshot) | `pre-task` (PreToolUse[Task]) | *(not used)* |
   430→| `SubagentEnd` | Extracts subagent modified files, detects changes, saves task checkpoint | `post-task` (PostToolUse[Task]) | *(not used)* |
   431→
   432→### Event Field Requirements
   433→
   434→| Event Type | Required Fields | Optional Fields |
   435→|------------|----------------|-----------------|
   436→| `SessionStart` | `SessionID`, `SessionRef` | `ResponseMessage` |
   437→| `TurnStart` | `SessionID`, `SessionRef` | `Prompt`, `PreviousSessionID` |
   438→| `TurnEnd` | `SessionID`, `SessionRef` | |
   439→| `Compaction` | `SessionID` | `SessionRef` |
   440→| `SessionEnd` | `SessionID` | `SessionRef` |
   441→| `SubagentStart` | `SessionID`, `SessionRef`, `ToolUseID` | `ToolInput` |
   442→| `SubagentEnd` | `SessionID`, `SessionRef`, `ToolUseID` | `SubagentID`, `ToolInput` |
   443→
   444→## Optional Interface Decision Tree
   445→
   446→### `TranscriptAnalyzer`
   447→
   448→**What it enables:** Transcript-derived file lists (more accurate than git-status-only), extracted user prompts in checkpoint metadata, session summaries.
   449→
   450→**Without it:** The framework still creates checkpoints using git-status-based file detection and stores the raw transcript. Prompts and summary fields will be empty.
   451→
   452→**Implement when:** Your agent writes a parseable transcript (JSONL, JSON, or any structured format) and you can extract which files were modified and what the user asked.
   453→
   454→**Methods:**
   455→- `GetTranscriptPosition(path) (int, error)` - Return current position. For JSONL: line count. For JSON with messages array: message count.
   456→- `ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path, startOffset) (files, currentPosition, error)` - Parse transcript from offset and return files touched by write/edit tools.
   457→- `ExtractPrompts(sessionRef, fromOffset) ([]string, error)` - Extract user prompt strings.
   458→- `ExtractSummary(sessionRef) (string, error)` - Extract last assistant response as summary.
   459→
   460→### `TranscriptPreparer`
   461→
   462→**What it enables:** A pre-read synchronization step before the framework reads the transcript.
   463→
   464→**Without it:** The framework reads the transcript immediately, which may be incomplete if the agent writes asynchronously.
   465→
   466→**Implement when:** Your agent writes transcripts asynchronously (e.g., Claude Code uses an async writer and needs to wait for a flush sentinel before reading).
   467→
   468→**Method:**
   469→- `PrepareTranscript(sessionRef) error` - Wait until the transcript is fully written. Called before `ReadTranscript`.
   470→
   471→### `TokenCalculator`
   472→
   473→**What it enables:** Token usage metrics in checkpoint metadata (input, output, cache tokens, API call count).
   474→
   475→**Without it:** Token usage fields are empty in checkpoint metadata.
   476→
   477→**Implement when:** Your agent's transcript contains token/usage data per message.
   478→
   479→**Method:**
   480→- `CalculateTokenUsage(sessionRef, fromOffset) (*TokenUsage, error)` - Sum token usage from offset to end of transcript.
   481→
   482→### `SubagentAwareExtractor`
   483→
   484→**What it enables:** Includes files modified by spawned subagents in the checkpoint file list and aggregates subagent token usage.
   485→
   486→**Without it:** Only the main agent's transcript is analyzed. Subagent modifications are still captured by git status but not attributed to the subagent.
   487→
   488→**Implement when:** Your agent spawns subagents (task workers) that have their own transcripts.
   489→
   490→**Methods:**
   491→- `ExtractAllModifiedFiles(sessionRef, fromOffset, subagentsDir) ([]string, error)` - Deduplicated file list from main + subagent transcripts.
   492→- `CalculateTotalTokenUsage(sessionRef, fromOffset, subagentsDir) (*TokenUsage, error)` - Aggregated usage including subagents.
   493→
   494→### `HookSupport`
   495→
   496→**What it enables:** `entire enable` automatically installs hooks into the agent's config file.
   497→
   498→**Without it:** Users must manually configure hooks to call `entire hooks <agent> <verb>`.
   499→
   500→**Implement when:** Your agent supports a config file with hook definitions (e.g., `.claude/settings.json`, `.gemini/settings.json`).
   501→
   502→### `FileWatcher`
   503→
   504→**What it enables:** Detecting session activity by watching file changes instead of hooks.
   505→
   506→**Without it:** The agent must support hooks for the framework to receive events.
   507→
   508→**Implement when:** Your agent doesn't support lifecycle hooks but writes session data to predictable file paths.
   509→
   510→## Transcript Format Guide
   511→
   512→### JSONL Format (Claude Code pattern)
   513→
   514→One JSON object per line. Each line is a transcript entry (user message, assistant message, tool use, etc.):
   515→
   516→```
   517→{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"Fix the bug"},"timestamp":"..."}
   518→{"type":"assistant","message":{"role":"assistant","content":[...]},"timestamp":"..."}
   519→```
   520→
   521→**Chunking:** Use `agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)` - splits at newline boundaries.
   522→**Reassembly:** Use `agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks)` - concatenates with newlines.
   523→**Position:** Line count (`bufio.Reader` + count `\n`).
   524→**Offset:** Start parsing at line N (skip first N lines).
   525→
   526→### JSON Format (Gemini CLI pattern)
   527→
   528→Single JSON object with a `messages` array:
   529→
   530→```json
   531→{"messages": [{"type": "user", "content": "..."}, {"type": "gemini", "content": "..."}]}
   532→```
   533→
   534→**Chunking:** Parse the JSON, split the messages array across chunks, marshal each chunk as a complete JSON object with a subset of messages.
   535→**Reassembly:** Parse each chunk, concatenate all message arrays, marshal back.
   536→**Position:** Message count (`len(transcript.Messages)`).
   537→**Offset:** Start iterating messages at index N.
   538→
   539→### Using Chunking Helpers
   540→
   541→The `agent` package provides format-agnostic entry points:
   542→
   543→```go
   544→// These dispatch to the agent's ChunkTranscript/ReassembleTranscript methods
   545→agent.ChunkTranscript(content, agentType)     // agentType → agent lookup → format-aware chunking
   546→agent.ReassembleTranscript(chunks, agentType)  // agentType → agent lookup → format-aware reassembly
   547→
   548→// Direct JSONL helpers (usable without an agent)
   549→agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   550→agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks)
   551→
   552→// Chunk file naming
   553→agent.ChunkFileName("full.jsonl", 0)  // "full.jsonl"
   554→agent.ChunkFileName("full.jsonl", 1)  // "full.jsonl.001"
   555→agent.ParseChunkIndex("full.jsonl.002", "full.jsonl")  // 2
   556→agent.SortChunkFiles(files, "full.jsonl")  // sorted by chunk index
   557→```
   558→
   559→## Hook Installation Patterns
   560→
   561→### JSON Config File Pattern
   562→
   563→Both Claude Code and Gemini CLI use a JSON settings file in their config directory. The installation pattern is:
   564→
   565→1. **Read existing settings** as `map[string]json.RawMessage` to preserve unknown fields
   566→2. **Parse only the hook types you modify** into typed slices
   567→3. **Remove existing Entire hooks** (for `force` mode or mode-switching)
   568→4. **Add new hooks** idempotently (check if command already exists)
   569→5. **Marshal modified types back** to the raw map
   570→6. **Write the file** with pretty-printing
   571→
   572→Key principles:
   573→- **Preserve unknown fields** - don't destroy user's custom hooks or settings
   574→- **Idempotent installs** - running `entire enable` twice doesn't duplicate hooks
   575→- **Support `localDev` mode** - use `go run ${PROJECT_DIR}/...` for development
   576→- **Identify Entire hooks** by command prefix (e.g., `"entire "` or `"go run ${...}"`)
   577→
   578→### Example: Claude Code Hook Config
   579→
   580→```json
   581→{
   582→  "hooks": {
   583→    "SessionStart": [{"matcher": "", "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "entire hooks claude-code session-start"}]}],
   584→    "Stop": [{"matcher": "", "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "entire hooks claude-code stop"}]}],
   585→    "PreToolUse": [{"matcher": "Task", "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "entire hooks claude-code pre-task"}]}]
   586→  }
   587→}
   588→```
   589→
   590→### Example: Gemini CLI Hook Config
   591→
   592→```json
   593→{
   594→  "hooksConfig": {"enabled": true},
   595→  "hooks": {
   596→    "SessionStart": [{"hooks": [{"name": "entire-session-start", "type": "command", "command": "entire hooks gemini session-start"}]}],
   597→    "AfterAgent": [{"hooks": [{"name": "entire-after-agent", "type": "command", "command": "entire hooks gemini after-agent"}]}]
   598→  }
   599→}
   600→```
   601→
   602→Note: Gemini CLI requires `hooksConfig.enabled: true` and each hook entry requires a `name` field.
   603→
   604→## Testing Patterns
   605→
   606→### Testing `ParseHookEvent`
   607→
   608→Test every hook name, including pass-through hooks that return nil:
   609→
   610→```go
   611→func TestParseHookEvent_TurnStart(t *testing.T) {
   612→    t.Parallel()
   613→
   614→    ag := &YourAgent{}
   615→    input := `{"session_id": "sess-123", "transcript_path": "/tmp/t.jsonl", "prompt": "Fix the bug"}`
   616→
   617→    event, err := ag.ParseHookEvent(HookNamePromptSubmit, strings.NewReader(input))
   618→
   619→    if err != nil {
   620→        t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   621→    }
   622→    if event == nil {
   623→        t.Fatal("expected event, got nil")
   624→    }
   625→    if event.Type != agent.TurnStart {
   626→        t.Errorf("expected TurnStart, got %v", event.Type)
   627→    }
   628→    if event.Prompt != "Fix the bug" {
   629→        t.Errorf("expected prompt 'Fix the bug', got %q", event.Prompt)
   630→    }
   631→}
   632→```
   633→
   634→### Testing Nil Returns
   635→
   636→Hooks with no lifecycle action must return `nil, nil`:
   637→
   638→```go
   639→func TestParseHookEvent_PassThrough_ReturnsNil(t *testing.T) {
   640→    t.Parallel()
   641→
   642→    ag := &YourAgent{}
   643→    event, err := ag.ParseHookEvent("some-pass-through-hook", strings.NewReader(`{}`))
   644→
   645→    if err != nil {
   646→        t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   647→    }
   648→    if event != nil {
   649→        t.Errorf("expected nil event, got %+v", event)
   650→    }
   651→}
   652→```
   653→
   654→### Testing Error Cases
   655→
   656→```go
   657→func TestParseHookEvent_EmptyInput(t *testing.T) {
   658→    t.Parallel()
   659→
   660→    ag := &YourAgent{}
   661→    _, err := ag.ParseHookEvent(HookNameSessionStart, strings.NewReader(""))
   662→
   663→    if err == nil {
   664→        t.Fatal("expected error for empty input")
   665→    }
   666→    if !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "empty hook input") {
   667→        t.Errorf("expected 'empty hook input' error, got: %v", err)
   668→    }
   669→}
   670→
   671→func TestParseHookEvent_MalformedJSON(t *testing.T) {
   672→    t.Parallel()
   673→
   674→    ag := &YourAgent{}
   675→    _, err := ag.ParseHookEvent(HookNameSessionStart, strings.NewReader("not json"))
   676→
   677→    if err == nil {
   678→        t.Fatal("expected error for malformed JSON")
   679→    }
   680→}
   681→```
   682→
   683→### Testing Hook Installation
   684→
   685→```go
   686→func TestInstallHooks_CreatesSettingsFile(t *testing.T) {
   687→    t.Parallel()
   688→
   689→    dir := t.TempDir()
   690→    // ... set up test repo, create .youragent/ directory ...
   691→
   692→    ag := &YourAgent{}
   693→    count, err := ag.InstallHooks(false, false)
   694→
   695→    if err != nil {
   696→        t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   697→    }
   698→    if count == 0 {
   699→        t.Error("expected hooks to be installed")
   700→    }
   701→
   702→    // Verify settings file was created and contains hooks
   703→    data, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(dir, ".youragent", "settings.json"))
   704→    // ... assert hooks are present ...
   705→}
   706→
   707→func TestInstallHooks_Idempotent(t *testing.T) {
   708→    t.Parallel()
   709→
   710→    // Install twice, second call should return count=0
   711→    ag := &YourAgent{}
   712→    ag.InstallHooks(false, false)
   713→    count, _ := ag.InstallHooks(false, false)
   714→    if count != 0 {
   715→        t.Errorf("expected 0 new hooks on second install, got %d", count)
   716→    }
   717→}
   718→```
   719→
   720→### Test file references
   721→
   722→- Claude Code lifecycle tests: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/lifecycle_test.go`
   723→- Claude Code hooks tests: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/hooks_test.go`
   724→- Claude Code transcript tests: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/transcript_test.go`
   725→- Gemini CLI lifecycle tests: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/lifecycle_test.go`
   726→- Gemini CLI hooks tests: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/hooks_test.go`
   727→- Gemini CLI transcript tests: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/transcript_test.go`
   728→
   729→## Common Pitfalls
   730→
   731→### go-git v5 Bugs
   732→
   733→**Do NOT use go-git v5 for `checkout` or `reset --hard` operations.** go-git v5 has a bug where `worktree.Reset()` with `HardReset` and `worktree.Checkout()` incorrectly delete untracked directories even when listed in `.gitignore`. This would destroy `.entire/` and agent config directories. Use the git CLI instead. See `CLAUDE.md` for details and `hard_reset_test.go` for regression tests.
   734→
   735→### Repo Root vs Current Working Directory
   736→
   737→Git commands return paths relative to the **repository root**, not the current working directory. When your code runs from a subdirectory, `os.Getwd()` gives wrong results for path construction. Always use `paths.RepoRoot()`:
   738→
   739→```go
   740→// WRONG
   741→cwd, _ := os.Getwd()
   742→absPath := filepath.Join(cwd, file) // Breaks from subdirectory
   743→
   744→// CORRECT
   745→repoRoot, _ := paths.RepoRoot()
   746→absPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, file)
   747→```
   748→
   749→### Transcript Flush Timing
   750→
   751→Some agents write transcripts asynchronously. If `ReadTranscript` is called before the write completes, the transcript will be incomplete. Implement `TranscriptPreparer` if your agent has this behavior. Claude Code solves this by writing a sentinel entry and polling for it (see `waitForTranscriptFlush` in `claudecode/lifecycle.go`).
   752→
   753→### Nil Event Return Pattern
   754→
   755→`ParseHookEvent` returning `(nil, nil)` is **not an error** - it means the hook has no lifecycle significance. The framework (in `hook_registry.go`) checks:
   756→
   757→```go
   758→event, parseErr := ag.ParseHookEvent(hookName, stdin)
   759→if event != nil {
   760→    hookErr = DispatchLifecycleEvent(ag, event)
   761→}
   762→// nil event → no-op (hook is silently acknowledged)
   763→```
   764→
   765→Use `//nolint:nilnil` to suppress the linter warning on intentional nil returns.
   766→
   767→### Agent Name vs Agent Type
   768→
   769→- `AgentName` is the **registry key** used in code (`"claude-code"`, `"gemini"`). It appears in CLI commands: `entire hooks claude-code stop`.
   770→- `AgentType` is the **display name** stored in metadata and commit trailers (`"Claude Code"`, `"Gemini CLI"`). It's what users see.
   771→
   772→Register constants for both in `cmd/entire/cli/agent/registry.go` when adding a new agent.
   773→
   774→### Hook Names as CLI Subcommands
   775→
   776→The strings returned by `HookNames()` become literal CLI subcommands under `entire hooks <agent>`. For example, if `HookNames()` returns `["session-start", "stop"]`, the CLI creates:
   777→- `entire hooks your-agent session-start`
   778→- `entire hooks your-agent stop`
   779→
   780→These commands read JSON from stdin and dispatch to `ParseHookEvent`. The agent's hook config should invoke these commands.
   781→
   782→## Complete Code Template
   783→
   784→A minimal but functional agent skeleton. Copy this directory structure and fill in agent-specific details:
   785→

   787→<summary>youragent/types.go</summary>
   788→
   789→```go
   790→package youragent
   791→
   792→// sessionInfoRaw matches your agent's session hook JSON payload.
   793→type sessionInfoRaw struct {
   794→	SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
   795→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
   796→}
   797→
   798→// promptInputRaw matches your agent's prompt-submit hook JSON payload.
   799→type promptInputRaw struct {
   800→	SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
   801→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
   802→	Prompt         string `json:"prompt"`
   803→}
   804→```

   809→<summary>youragent/youragent.go</summary>

   811→```go
   812→package youragent
   813→
   814→import (
   815→	"errors"
   816→	"fmt"
   817→	"io"
   818→	"os"
   819→	"path/filepath"
   820→
   821→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
   822→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
   823→)
   824→
   825→//nolint:gochecknoinits // Agent self-registration is the intended pattern
   826→func init() {
   827→	agent.Register("your-agent", NewYourAgent)
   828→}
   829→
   830→type YourAgent struct{}
   831→
   832→func NewYourAgent() agent.Agent { return &YourAgent{} }
   833→
   834→func (a *YourAgent) Name() agent.AgentName  { return "your-agent" }
   835→func (a *YourAgent) Type() agent.AgentType   { return "Your Agent" }
   836→func (a *YourAgent) Description() string     { return "Your Agent - brief description" }
   837→func (a *YourAgent) ProtectedDirs() []string { return []string{".youragent"} }
   838→
   839→func (a *YourAgent) DetectPresence() (bool, error) {
   840→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   841→	if err != nil {
   842→		repoRoot = "."
   843→	}
   844→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".youragent")); err == nil {
   845→		return true, nil
   846→	}
   847→	return false, nil
   848→}
   849→
   850→func (a *YourAgent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {
   851→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path from agent hook
   852→	if err != nil {
   853→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   854→	}
   855→	return data, nil
   856→}
   857→
   858→func (a *YourAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
   859→	return agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   860→}
   861→
   862→func (a *YourAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
   863→	return agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks), nil
   864→}
   865→
   866→// --- Session Management ---
   867→func (a *YourAgent) GetHookConfigPath() string                                          { return "" }
   868→func (a *YourAgent) SupportsHooks() bool                                                { return true }
   869→func (a *YourAgent) ParseHookInput(_ agent.HookType, r io.Reader) (*agent.HookInput, error) {
   870→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](r)
   871→	if err != nil {
   872→		return nil, err
   873→	}
   874→	return &agent.HookInput{SessionID: raw.SessionID, SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath}, nil
   875→}
   876→func (a *YourAgent) GetSessionID(input *agent.HookInput) string                         { return input.SessionID }
   877→func (a *YourAgent) GetSessionDir(_ string) (string, error)                             { return "", errors.New("not implemented") }
   878→func (a *YourAgent) ResolveSessionFile(dir, id string) string                           { return filepath.Join(dir, id+".jsonl") }
   879→func (a *YourAgent) ReadSession(_ *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error)        { return nil, errors.New("not implemented") }
   880→func (a *YourAgent) WriteSession(_ *agent.AgentSession) error                           { return errors.New("not implemented") }
   881→func (a *YourAgent) FormatResumeCommand(id string) string                               { return "youragent --resume " + id }
   882→```

   886→<details>
   887→<summary>youragent/lifecycle.go</summary>
   888→
   889→```go
   890→package youragent
   891→
   892→import (
   893→	"io"
   894→	"time"
   895→
   896→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
   897→)
   898→
   899→const (
   900→	HookNameSessionStart = "session-start"
   901→	HookNameSessionEnd   = "session-end"
   902→	HookNamePromptSubmit = "prompt-submit"
   903→	HookNameResponse     = "response"
   904→)
   905→
   906→func (a *YourAgent) HookNames() []string {
   907→	return []string{
   908→		HookNameSessionStart,
   909→		HookNameSessionEnd,
   910→		HookNamePromptSubmit,
   911→		HookNameResponse,
   912→	}
   913→}
   914→
   915→func (a *YourAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   916→	switch hookName {
   917→	case HookNameSessionStart:
   918→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   919→		if err != nil {
   920→			return nil, err
   921→		}
   922→		return &agent.Event{
   923→			Type:       agent.SessionStart,
   924→			SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   925→			SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   926→			Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   927→		}, nil
   928→
   929→	case HookNamePromptSubmit:
   930→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[promptInputRaw](stdin)
   931→		if err != nil {
   932→			return nil, err
   933→		}
   934→		return &agent.Event{
   935→			Type:       agent.TurnStart,
   936→			SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   937→			SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   938→			Prompt:     raw.Prompt,
   939→			Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   940→		}, nil
   941→
   942→	case HookNameResponse:
   943→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   944→		if err != nil {
   945→			return nil, err
   946→		}
   947→		return &agent.Event{
   948→			Type:       agent.TurnEnd,
   949→			SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   950→			SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   951→			Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   952→		}, nil
   953→
   954→	case HookNameSessionEnd:
   955→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   956→		if err != nil {
   957→			return nil, err
   958→		}
   959→		return &agent.Event{
   960→			Type:       agent.SessionEnd,
   961→			SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   962→			SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   963→			Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   964→		}, nil
   965→
   966→	default:
   967→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action
   968→	}
   969→}
   970→```
   971→
   972→</details>

   978→package youragent

   987→func TestParseHookEvent_SessionStart(t *testing.T) {
   988→	t.Parallel()
   989→
   990→	ag := &YourAgent{}
   991→	input := `{"session_id": "test-session", "transcript_path": "/tmp/transcript.jsonl"}`
   992→
   993→	event, err := ag.ParseHookEvent(HookNameSessionStart, strings.NewReader(input))
   994→
   995→	if err != nil {
   996→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   997→	}
   998→	if event == nil {
   999→		t.Fatal("expected event, got nil")
  1000→	}
  1001→	if event.Type != agent.SessionStart {
  1002→		t.Errorf("expected SessionStart, got %v", event.Type)
  1003→	}
  1004→	if event.SessionID != "test-session" {
  1005→		t.Errorf("expected session_id 'test-session', got %q", event.SessionID)
  1006→	}
  1007→}
  1008→
  1009→func TestParseHookEvent_TurnStart(t *testing.T) {
  1010→	t.Parallel()
  1011→
  1012→	ag := &YourAgent{}
  1013→	input := `{"session_id": "sess-1", "transcript_path": "/tmp/t.jsonl", "prompt": "Hello"}`
  1014→
  1015→	event, err := ag.ParseHookEvent(HookNamePromptSubmit, strings.NewReader(input))
  1016→
  1017→	if err != nil {
  1018→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
  1019→	}
  1020→	if event.Type != agent.TurnStart {
  1021→		t.Errorf("expected TurnStart, got %v", event.Type)
  1022→	}
  1023→	if event.Prompt != "Hello" {
  1024→		t.Errorf("expected prompt 'Hello', got %q", event.Prompt)
  1025→	}
  1026→}
  1027→
  1028→func TestParseHookEvent_TurnEnd(t *testing.T) {
  1029→	t.Parallel()
  1030→
  1031→	ag := &YourAgent{}
  1032→	input := `{"session_id": "sess-2", "transcript_path": "/tmp/t.jsonl"}`
  1033→

  1035→
  1036→	if err != nil {

  1038→	}

  1043→
  1044→func TestParseHookEvent_SessionEnd(t *testing.T) {

  1046→
  1047→	ag := &YourAgent{}

  1049→

  1059→
  1060→func TestParseHookEvent_UnknownHook(t *testing.T) {

`json:\"tool_use_id\"`\n\tToolInput      json.RawMessage `json:\"tool_input,omitempty\"`\n}\n\n// postTaskHookInputRaw matches OpenCode's task-complete hook JSON payload.\ntype postTaskHookInputRaw \"user\"\n\tMessageTypeAssistant = \"assistant\"\n\tMessageTypeTool      = \"tool\"\n)\n\n// TranscriptEntry represents a single entry in OpenCode's JSONL transcript.\ntype TranscriptEntry struct {\n\tType      string          `json:\"type\"`\n\tMessage   json.RawMessage `json:\"message\"`\n\tTimestamp string          `json:\"timestamp,omitempty\"`\n}\n\n// UserMessage represents the content `json:\"usage,omitempty\"`\n}\n\n// ContentBlock represents a content block in an assistant message.\ntype ContentBlock struct {\n\tType  string `json:\"type\"`\n\tText  string `json:\"text,omitempty\"`\n\tName  string `json:\"name,omitempty\"`\n\tInput any    `json:\"input,omitempty\"`\n}\n\n// TokenUsageEntry represents token usage data from OpenCode's transcript.\ntype TokenUsageEntry struct {\n\tInputTokens  int `json:\"input_tokens\"`\n\tOutputTokens int `json:\"output_tokens\"`\n\tCacheRead    int `json:\"cache_read_input_tokens,omitempty\"`\n\tCacheCreate  int (\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"io\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths\"\n)\n\n//nolint:gochecknoinits // Agent self-registration is the intended pattern\nfunc init() {\n\tagent.Register(agent.AgentNameOpenCode, NewOpenCodeAgent)\n}\n\n// OpenCodeAgent implements the Agent interface for OpenCode.\n//\n//nolint:revive // OpenCodeAgent is clearer than Agent in this context\ntype OpenCodeAgent struct{}\n\n// NewOpenCodeAgent creates a new OpenCode agent instance.\nfunc NewOpenCodeAgent() agent.Agent {\n\treturn &OpenCodeAgent{}\n}\n\n// --- Identity ---\n\n// Name returns the agent registry key.\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) Name() agent.AgentName {\n\treturn agent.AgentNameOpenCode\n}\n\n// Type returns the agent type identifier stored in metadata/trailers.\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) Type() agent.AgentType {\n\treturn agent.AgentTypeOpenCode\n}\n\n// Description returns a human-readable description.\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) Description() string {\n\treturn \"OpenCode - AI coding CLI\"\n}\n\n// DetectPresence checks if OpenCode is configured in the repository.\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) DetectPresence() (bool, error) {\n\trepoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\trepoRoot = \".\"\n\t}\n\n\t// Check for .opencode directory\n\tif _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(repoRoot, \".opencode\")); err == nil {\n\t\treturn true, nil\n\t}\n\t// Check for opencode.json config\n\tif _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(repoRoot, \"opencode.json\")); err == nil {\n\t\treturn true, nil\n\t}\n\treturn false, nil\n}\n\n// ProtectedDirs returns directories that OpenCode uses for config/state.\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) ProtectedDirs() []string { return []string{\".opencode\"} }\n\n// --- Transcript Storage ---\n\n// ReadTranscript reads the raw JSONL transcript bytes for a session.\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read transcript: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn data, nil\n}\n\n// ChunkTranscript splits a transcript into chunks using JSONL line splitting.\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {\n\treturn agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)\n}\n\n// ReassembleTranscript combines chunks back into a single transcript.\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {\n\treturn agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks), nil\n}\n\n// --- Session Management (legacy methods) ---\n\n// GetHookConfigPath returns the path to OpenCode's hook config.\n// OpenCode uses a TypeScript plugin, not a JSON config.\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) GetHookConfigPath() string { return \"\" }\n\n// SupportsHooks returns true as OpenCode supports lifecycle hooks via plugin.\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) SupportsHooks() bool { return true }\n\n// ParseHookInput parses OpenCode hook input from stdin (legacy interface).\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) ParseHookInput(_ agent.HookType, r io.Reader) (*agent.HookInput, error) {\n\traw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](r)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &agent.HookInput{\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.SessionID,\n\t\tSessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,\n\t}, nil\n}\n\n// GetSessionID extracts the session ID from hook input.\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) GetSessionID(input *agent.HookInput) string {\n\treturn input.SessionID\n}\n\n// GetSessionDir returns the directory where OpenCode stores session data.\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) GetSessionDir(_ string) (string, error) {\n\treturn \"\", errors.New(\"not implemented\")\n}\n\n// ResolveSessionFile returns the path to a session transcript file.\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string {\n\treturn filepath.Join(sessionDir, agentSessionID+\".jsonl\")\n}\n\n// ReadSession reads session data from OpenCode's storage.\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) ReadSession(input *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {\n\tif input.SessionRef == \"\" {\n\t\treturn nil, errors.New(\"session reference (transcript path) is required\")\n\t}\n\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(input.SessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from trusted hook input\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read transcript: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn &agent.AgentSession{\n\t\tSessionID:     input.SessionID,\n\t\tAgentName:     o.Name(),\n\t\tSessionRef:    input.SessionRef,\n\t\tNativeData:    data,\n\t\tModifiedFiles: []string{},\n\t}, nil\n}\n\n// WriteSession writes session data (no-op for OpenCode - plugin handles exports).\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) WriteSession(session *agent.AgentSession) error {\n\tif session == nil {\n\t\treturn errors.New(\"session is nil\")\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// FormatResumeCommand returns the command to resume an OpenCode session.\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string {\n\treturn \"opencode --session \" + sessionID\n}\n\n// --- TranscriptAnalyzer interface ---\n\n// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current line count of the JSONL transcript.\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {\n\tif path == \"\" {\n\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t}\n\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif os.IsNotExist(err) {\n\t\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read transcript: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn countJSONLLines(data), nil\n}\n\n// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given line offset.\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error) {\n\tif path == \"\" {\n\t\treturn nil, 0, nil\n\t}\n\n\tdata, readErr := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input\n\tif readErr != nil {\n\t\tif os.IsNotExist(readErr) {\n\t\t\treturn nil, 0, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn nil, 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read transcript: %w\", readErr)\n\t}\n\n\tentries := parseJSONLEntries(data)\n\ttotalLines := len(entries)\n\n\tfileSet := make(map[string]bool)\n\tfor i := startOffset; i < len(entries); i++ {\n\t\textractFilesFromEntry(entries[i], fileSet)\n\t}\n\n\tfor f := range fileSet {\n\t\tfiles = append(files, f)\n\t}\n\n\treturn files, totalLines, nil\n}\n\n// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompts from the transcript starting at the given line offset.\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error) {\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read transcript: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tentries := parseJSONLEntries(data)\n\n\tvar prompts []string\n\tfor i := fromOffset; i < len(entries); i++ {\n\t\tif entries[i].Type != MessageTypeUser {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tvar msg UserMessage\n\t\tif json.Unmarshal(entries[i].Message, &msg) == nil && msg.Content != \"\" {\n\t\t\tprompts = append(prompts, msg.Content)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn prompts, nil\n}\n\n// ExtractSummary extracts the last assistant message as a session summary.\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error) {\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read transcript: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tentries := parseJSONLEntries(data)\n\n\t// Walk backward to find last assistant text block\n\tfor i := len(entries) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {\n\t\tif entries[i].Type != MessageTypeAssistant {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tvar msg AssistantMessage\n\t\tif json.Unmarshal(entries[i].Message, &msg) != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, block := range msg.Content {\n\t\t\tif block.Type == \"text\" && block.Text != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\treturn block.Text, nil\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn \"\", nil\n}\n\n// --- TokenCalculator interface ---\n\n// CalculateTokenUsage computes token usage from the transcript starting at the given line offset.\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) CalculateTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read transcript: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tentries := parseJSONLEntries(data)\n\tusage := &agent.TokenUsage{}\n\n\tfor i := fromOffset; i < len(entries); i++ {\n\t\tif entries[i].Type != MessageTypeAssistant {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tvar msg AssistantMessage\n\t\tif json.Unmarshal(entries[i].Message, &msg) != nil || msg.Usage == nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tvar tu TokenUsageEntry\n\t\tif json.Unmarshal(*msg.Usage, &tu) != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tusage.InputTokens += tu.InputTokens\n\t\tusage.OutputTokens += tu.OutputTokens\n\t\tusage.CacheReadTokens += tu.CacheRead\n\t\tusage.CacheCreateTokens += tu.CacheCreate\n\t\tusage.APICalls++\n\t}\n\n\treturn usage, nil\n}\n\n// --- Internal helpers ---\n\n// countJSONLLines counts non-empty lines in JSONL content.\nfunc countJSONLLines(data []byte) int {\n\tcount := 0\n\tstart := 0\n\tfor i := 0; i < len(data); i++ {\n\t\tif data[i] == '\\n' {\n\t\t\tif i > start {\n\t\t\t\tcount++\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tstart = i + 1\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\t// Handle last line without trailing newline\n\tif start < len(data) {\n\t\tcount++\n\t}\n\treturn count\n}\n\n// parseJSONLEntries parses JSONL content into transcript entries.\nfunc parseJSONLEntries(data []byte) []TranscriptEntry {\n\tvar entries []TranscriptEntry\n\tstart := 0\n\tfor i := 0; i <= len(data); i++ {\n\t\tif i == len(data) || data[i] == '\\n' {\n\t\t\tline := data[start:i]\n\t\t\tstart = i + 1\n\t\t\tif len(line) == 0 {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tvar entry TranscriptEntry\n\t\t\tif json.Unmarshal(line, &entry) == nil {\n\t\t\t\tentries = append(entries, entry)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn entries\n}\n\n// extractFilesFromEntry extracts file paths from assistant tool-use content blocks.\nfunc extractFilesFromEntry(entry TranscriptEntry, fileSet map[string]bool) {\n\tif entry.Type != MessageTypeAssistant {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tvar msg AssistantMessage\n\tif json.Unmarshal(entry.Message, &msg) != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, block := range msg.Content {\n\t\tif block.Type != \"tool_use\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tisModifyTool := false\n\t\tfor _, name := range FileModificationTools {\n\t\t\tif block.Name == name {\n\t\t\t\tisModifyTool = true\n\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif !isModifyTool {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Extract file_path from input map\n\t\tinputMap, ok := block.Input.(map[string]any)\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif fp, ok := inputMap[\"file_path\"].(string); ok && fp != \"\" && !fileSet[fp] {\n\t\t\tfileSet[fp] = true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n"
}

= (*OpenCodeAgent)(nil)\n\t_ agent.TokenCalculator    = (*OpenCodeAgent)(nil)\n)\n\n// Hook name constants - these become subcommands under `entire hooks opencode`\nconst (\n\tHookNameSessionStart = \"session-start\"\n\tHookNamePromptSubmit = \"prompt-submit\"\n\tHookNameStop         = \"stop\"\n\tHookNameSessionEnd   = \"session-end\"\n\tHookNameTaskStart    = \"task-start\"\n\tHookNameTaskComplete = \"task-complete\"\n)\n\n// HookNames returns the hook verbs OpenCode supports.\n// Delegates to GetHookNames for backward compatibility.\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) HookNames() []string {\n\treturn o.GetHookNames()\n}\n\n// ParseHookEvent translates an OpenCode hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.\n// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance.\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\tswitch hookName {\n\tcase HookNameSessionStart:\n\t\treturn o.parseSessionStart(stdin)\n\tcase HookNamePromptSubmit:\n\t\treturn o.parseTurnStart(stdin)\n\tcase HookNameStop:\n\t\treturn o.parseTurnEnd(stdin)\n\tcase HookNameSessionEnd:\n\t\treturn o.parseSessionEnd(stdin)\n\tcase HookNameTaskStart:\n\t\treturn o.parseSubagentStart(stdin)\n\tcase HookNameTaskComplete:\n\t\treturn o.parseSubagentEnd(stdin)\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action\n\t}\n}\n\n// --- Internal hook parsing functions ---\n\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) parseSessionStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\traw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &agent.Event{\n\t\tType:       agent.SessionStart,\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.SessionID,\n\t\tSessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,\n\t\tTimestamp:  time.Now(),\n\t}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) parseTurnStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\traw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[promptInputRaw](stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &agent.Event{\n\t\tType:       agent.TurnStart,\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.SessionID,\n\t\tSessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,\n\t\tPrompt:     raw.Prompt,\n\t\tTimestamp:  time.Now(),\n\t}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) parseTurnEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\traw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &agent.Event{\n\t\tType:       agent.TurnEnd,\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.SessionID,\n\t\tSessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,\n\t\tTimestamp:  time.Now(),\n\t}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) parseSessionEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\traw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &agent.Event{\n\t\tType:       agent.SessionEnd,\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.SessionID,\n\t\tSessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,\n\t\tTimestamp:  time.Now(),\n\t}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) parseSubagentStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\traw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[taskHookInputRaw](stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &agent.Event{\n\t\tType:       agent.SubagentStart,\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.SessionID,\n\t\tSessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,\n\t\tToolUseID:  raw.ToolUseID,\n\t\tToolInput:  raw.ToolInput,\n\t\tTimestamp:  time.Now(),\n\t}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) parseSubagentEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\traw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[postTaskHookInputRaw](stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tevent := &agent.Event{\n\t\tType:       agent.SubagentEnd,\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.SessionID,\n\t\tSessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,\n\t\tToolUseID:  raw.ToolUseID,\n\t\tToolInput:  raw.ToolInput,\n\t\tTimestamp:  time.Now(),\n\t}\n\tif raw.AgentID != \"\" {\n\t\tevent.SubagentID = raw.AgentID\n\t}\n\treturn event, nil\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/opencode/entire.ts",
  "content": "// Entire integration plugin for OpenCode.\n// Installed by `entire enable --agent opencode` into .opencode/plugins/entire.ts\n//\n// This plugin listens for OpenCode lifecycle events and calls the Entire CLI\n// hook commands to create session checkpoints.\n\nimport type { Plugin } from \"opencode\";\n\nconst ENTIRE_BIN = \"__ENTIRE_BIN__\";\n\nfunction callHook(verb: string, payload: Record<string, unknown>): void {\n  const proc = Bun.spawn([...ENTIRE_BIN.split(\" \"), \"hooks\", \"opencode\", verb], {\n    stdin: new Blob([JSON.stringify(payload)]),\n    stdout: \"ignore\",\n    stderr: \"ignore\",\n  });\n  // Fire-and-forget: don't await, don't block the agent\n  proc.exited.catch(() => {});\n}\n\nexport default {\n  name: \"entire\",\n  setup(app) {\n    // Session lifecycle\n    app.on(\"session.created\", (session) => {\n      callHook(\"session-start\", {\n        session_id: session.id,\n        transcript_path: session.transcript ?? \"\",\n      });\n    });\n\n    // Prompt submit — fires on every user message\n    app.on(\"message.updated\", (message) => {\n      if (message.role !== \"user\") return;\n      callHook(\"prompt-submit\", {\n        session_id: message.sessionID ?? \"\",\n        transcript_path: message.transcript ?? \"\",\n        prompt: typeof message.content === \"string\" ? message.content : \"\",\n      });\n    });\n\n    // Agent idle — equivalent to Claude Code's \"stop\" hook\n    app.on(\"session.idle\", (session) => {\n      callHook(\"stop\", {\n        session_id: session.id,\n        transcript_path: session.transcript ?? \"\",\n      });\n    });\n\n    // Session compacted — context window trimmed\n    app.on(\"session.compacted\", (session) => {\n      callHook(\"session-end\", {\n        session_id: session.id,\n        transcript_path: session.transcript ?? \"\",\n      });\n    });\n\n    // Task (subagent) lifecycle\n    app.on(\"tool.execute.before\", (tool) => {\n      if (tool.name !== \"task\" && tool.name !== \"Task\") return;\n      callHook(\"task-start\", {\n        session_id: tool.sessionID ?? \"\",\n        transcript_path: tool.transcript ?? \"\",\n        tool_use_id: tool.id ?? \"\",\n        tool_input: tool.input ?? {},\n      });\n    });\n\n    app.on(\"tool.execute.after\", (tool) => {\n      if (tool.name !== \"task\" && tool.name !== \"Task\") return;\n      callHook(\"task-complete\", {\n        session_id: tool.sessionID ?? \"\",\n        transcript_path: tool.transcript ?? \"\",\n        tool_use_id: tool.id ?? \"\",\n        tool_input: tool.input ?? {},\n        agent_id: tool.agentID ?? \"\",\n      });\n    });\n  },\n} satisfies Plugin;\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/opencode/hooks.go",
  "content": "package opencode\n\nimport (\n\t\"embed\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths\"\n)\n\n// Ensure OpenCodeAgent implements HookSupport and HookHandler.\nvar (\n\t_ agent.HookSupport = (*OpenCodeAgent)(nil)\n\t_ agent.HookHandler = (*OpenCodeAgent)(nil)\n)\n\n//go:embed entire.ts\nvar pluginFS embed.FS\n\n// pluginFileName is the name of the embedded plugin file.\nconst pluginFileName = \"entire.ts\"\n\n// pluginInstallDir is the directory within .opencode where the plugin is installed.\nconst pluginInstallDir = \"plugins\"\n\n// entireHookPrefixes are command prefixes that identify Entire hooks in the plugin.\nvar entireHookPrefixes = []string{\n\t\"entire \",\n\t\"go run \",\n}\n\n// GetHookNames returns the hook verbs OpenCode supports.\n// These become subcommands: entire hooks opencode <verb>\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) GetHookNames() []string {\n\treturn []string{\n\t\tHookNameSessionStart,\n\t\tHookNamePromptSubmit,\n\t\tHookNameStop,\n\t\tHookNameSessionEnd,\n\t\tHookNameTaskStart,\n\t\tHookNameTaskComplete,\n\t}\n}\n\n// InstallHooks installs the Entire plugin for OpenCode.\n// The plugin is a TypeScript file installed to .opencode/plugins/entire.ts.\n// If force is true, overwrites the existing plugin file.\n// Returns the number of hooks installed (1 plugin = all hooks).\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error) {\n\trepoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\trepoRoot, err = os.Getwd() //nolint:forbidigo // Intentional fallback when RepoRoot() fails (tests)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get current directory: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tpluginDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, \".opencode\", pluginInstallDir)\n\tpluginPath := filepath.Join(pluginDir, pluginFileName)\n\n\t// Check idempotency - if plugin exists with same mode, skip\n\tif !force {\n\t\tif existing, err := os.ReadFile(pluginPath); err == nil { //nolint:gosec // path constructed from repo root\n\t\t\texpectedBin := entireBinPrefix(localDev)\n\t\t\tif strings.Contains(string(existing), expectedBin) {\n\t\t\t\treturn 0, nil // Already installed with same mode\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Read embedded plugin template\n\ttemplateBytes, err := pluginFS.ReadFile(pluginFileName)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read embedded plugin: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Replace __ENTIRE_BIN__ placeholder with the actual command\n\tbin := entireBinPrefix(localDev)\n\tcontent := strings.ReplaceAll(string(templateBytes), \"__ENTIRE_BIN__\", bin)\n\n\t// Create directory and write plugin\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(pluginDir, 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create plugins directory: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(pluginPath, []byte(content), 0o600); err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to write plugin file: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// 6 hook verbs are registered via the plugin\n\treturn 6, nil\n}\n\n// UninstallHooks removes the Entire plugin from OpenCode.\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) UninstallHooks() error {\n\trepoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\trepoRoot = \".\"\n\t}\n\n\tpluginPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, \".opencode\", pluginInstallDir, pluginFileName)\n\tif err := os.Remove(pluginPath); err != nil && !os.IsNotExist(err) {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to remove plugin: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// AreHooksInstalled checks if the Entire plugin is installed.\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) AreHooksInstalled() bool {\n\trepoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\trepoRoot = \".\"\n\t}\n\n\tpluginPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, \".opencode\", pluginInstallDir, pluginFileName)\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(pluginPath) //nolint:gosec // path constructed from repo root\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\n\t// Check if it's our plugin by looking for the Entire hook command\n\tcontent := string(data)\n\tfor _, prefix := range entireHookPrefixes {\n\t\tif strings.Contains(content, prefix) {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\n// GetSupportedHooks returns the hook types OpenCode supports.\nfunc (o *OpenCodeAgent) GetSupportedHooks() []agent.HookType {\n\treturn []agent.HookType{\n\t\tagent.HookSessionStart,\n\t\tagent.HookUserPromptSubmit,\n\t\tagent.HookStop,\n\t\tagent.HookSessionEnd,\n\t\tagent.HookPreToolUse,\n\t\tagent.HookPostToolUse,\n\t}\n}\n\n// entireBinPrefix returns the command prefix for the Entire binary.\nfunc entireBinPrefix(localDev bool) string {\n\tif localDev {\n\t\treturn \"go run {

  "file_path": "/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/setup.go",
  "old_string": "// removeAgentHooks removes hooks from all agents that support hooks.\nfunc removeAgentHooks(w io.Writer) error {\n\tvar errs []error\n\n\t// Remove Claude Code hooks\n\tclaudeAgent, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameClaudeCode)\n\tif err == nil {\n\t\tif hookAgent, ok := claudeAgent.(agent.HookSupport); ok {\n\t\t\twasInstalled := hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()\n\t\t\tif err := hookAgent.UninstallHooks(); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\terrs = append(errs, err)\n\t\t\t} else if wasInstalled {\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintln(w, \"  Removed Claude Code hooks\")\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Remove Gemini CLI hooks\n\tgeminiAgent, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameGemini)\n\tif err == nil {\n\t\tif hookAgent, ok := geminiAgent.(agent.HookSupport); ok {\n\t\t\twasInstalled := hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()\n\t\t\tif err := hookAgent.UninstallHooks(); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\terrs = append(errs, err)\n\t\t\t} else if wasInstalled {\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintln(w, \"  Removed Gemini CLI hooks\")\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn errors.Join(errs...)\n}",
  "new_string": "// removeAgentHooks removes hooks from all agents that support hooks.\nfunc removeAgentHooks(w io.Writer) error {\n\tvar errs []error\n\n\tfor _, agentName := range agent.List() {\n\t\tag, err := agent.Get(agentName)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\thookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\twasInstalled := hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()\n\t\tif err := hookAgent.UninstallHooks(); err != nil {\n\t\t\terrs = append(errs, err)\n\t\t} else if wasInstalled {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"  Removed %s hooks\\n\", ag.Type())\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn errors.Join(errs...)\n}"
}

  "new_string": "\t// Gather counts for display\n\tsessionStateCount := countSessionStates()\n\tshadowBranchCount := countShadowBranches()\n\tgitHooksInstalled := strategy.IsGitHookInstalled()\n\tagentsWithHooks := getAgentNamesWithHooksInstalled()\n\tentireDirExists := checkEntireDirExists()\n\n\t// Check if there's anything to uninstall\n\tif !entireDirExists && !gitHooksInstalled && sessionStateCount == 0 &&\n\t\tshadowBranchCount == 0 && len(agentsWithHooks) == 0 {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintln(w, \"Entire is not installed in this repository.\")\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Show confirmation prompt unless --force\n\tif !force {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintln(w, \"\\nThis will completely remove Entire from this repository:\")\n\t\tif entireDirExists {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintln(w, \"  - .entire/ directory\")\n\t\t}\n\t\tif gitHooksInstalled {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintln(w, \"  - Git hooks (prepare-commit-msg, commit-msg, post-commit, pre-push)\")\n\t\t}\n\t\tif sessionStateCount > 0 {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"  - Session state files (%d)\\n\", sessionStateCount)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif shadowBranchCount > 0 {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"  - Shadow branches (%d)\\n\", shadowBranchCount)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif len(agentsWithHooks) > 0 {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"  - Agent hooks (%s)\\n\", strings.Join(agentsWithHooks, \", \"))\n\t\t}"

  "old_string": "// checkClaudeCodeHooksInstalled checks if Claude Code hooks are installed.\nfunc checkClaudeCodeHooksInstalled() bool {\n\tag, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameClaudeCode)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\thookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\treturn hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()\n}\n\n// checkGeminiCLIHooksInstalled checks if Gemini CLI hooks are installed.\nfunc checkGeminiCLIHooksInstalled() bool {\n\tag, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameGemini)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\thookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\treturn hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()\n}",
  "new_string": "// getAgentNamesWithHooksInstalled returns display names of agents that have hooks installed.\nfunc getAgentNamesWithHooksInstalled() []string {\n\tvar names []string\n\tfor _, agentName := range agent.List() {\n\t\tag, err := agent.Get(agentName)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif hs, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport); ok && hs.AreHooksInstalled() {\n\t\t\tnames = append(names, string(ag.Type()))\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn names\n}"
}

{
