  "content": "package opencode\n\nimport (\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\t\"testing\"\n)\n\nfunc TestInstallHooks_CreatesPluginFile(t *testing.T) {\n\tdir := t.TempDir()\n\tt.Chdir(dir)\n\n\t// Initialize a git repo so paths.RepoRoot() works\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(dir, \".git\"), 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to create .git: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tag := &OpenCodeAgent{}\n\tcount, err := ag.InstallHooks(false, false)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"unexpected error: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif count == 0 {\n\t\tt.Error(\"expected hooks to be installed\")\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify plugin file was created\n\tpluginPath := filepath.Join(dir, \".opencode\", \"plugins\", \"entire.ts\")\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(pluginPath)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"plugin file not created: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Check it contains the production command\n\tcontent := string(data)\n\tif !contains(content, `\"entire\"`) {\n\t\tt.Error(\"expected plugin to contain 'entire' command\")\n\t}\n\tif contains(content, \"go run\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"expected production mode, but found go run prefix\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestInstallHooks_LocalDev(t *testing.T) {\n\tdir := t.TempDir()\n\tt.Chdir(dir)\n\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(dir, \".git\"), 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to create .git: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tag := &OpenCodeAgent{}\n\tcount, err := ag.InstallHooks(true, false)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"unexpected error: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif count == 0 {\n\t\tt.Error(\"expected hooks to be installed\")\n\t}\n\n\tpluginPath := filepath.Join(dir, \".opencode\", \"plugins\", \"entire.ts\")\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(pluginPath)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"plugin file not created: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif !contains(string(data), \"go run\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"expected local dev mode to use go run prefix\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestInstallHooks_Idempotent(t *testing.T) {\n\tdir := t.TempDir()\n\tt.Chdir(dir)\n\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(dir, \".git\"), 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to create .git: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tag := &OpenCodeAgent{}\n\n\t// First install\n\tcount1, err := ag.InstallHooks(false, false)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"first install error: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif count1 == 0 {\n\t\tt.Error(\"expected hooks installed on first call\")\n\t}\n\n\t// Second install should be idempotent\n\tcount2, err := ag.InstallHooks(false, false)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"second install error: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif count2 != 0 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected 0 new hooks on second install, got %d\", count2)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestInstallHooks_ForceReinstall(t *testing.T) {\n\tdir := t.TempDir()\n\tt.Chdir(dir)\n\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(dir, \".git\"), 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to create .git: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tag := &OpenCodeAgent{}\n\n\t// First install\n\tif _, err := ag.InstallHooks(false, false); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"first install error: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Force reinstall\n\tcount, err := ag.InstallHooks(false, true)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"force install error: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif count == 0 {\n\t\tt.Error(\"expected hooks reinstalled with force\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestUninstallHooks(t *testing.T) {\n\tdir := t.TempDir()\n\tt.Chdir(dir)\n\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(dir, \".git\"), 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to create .git: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tag := &OpenCodeAgent{}\n\n\t// Install first\n\tif _, err := ag.InstallHooks(false, false); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"install error: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify installed\n\tif !ag.AreHooksInstalled() {\n\t\tt.Error(\"expected hooks to be installed\")\n\t}\n\n\t// Uninstall\n\tif err := ag.UninstallHooks(); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"uninstall error: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify uninstalled\n\tif ag.AreHooksInstalled() {\n\t\tt.Error(\"expected hooks to be uninstalled\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestAreHooksInstalled_NoFile(t *testing.T) {\n\tdir := t.TempDir()\n\tt.Chdir(dir)\n\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(dir, \".git\"), 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to create .git: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tag := &OpenCodeAgent{}\n\tif ag.AreHooksInstalled() {\n\t\tt.Error(\"expected hooks not installed when no plugin file exists\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestGetSupportedHooks(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\tag := &OpenCodeAgent{}\n\thooks := ag.GetSupportedHooks()\n\tif len(hooks) == 0 {\n\t\tt.Error(\"expected non-empty supported hooks\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestGetHookNames(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\tag := &OpenCodeAgent{}\n\tnames := ag.GetHookNames()\n\n\tif len(names) != 6 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected 6 hook names, got %d\", len(names))\n\t}\n}\n\n// contains checks if s contains substr.\nfunc contains(s, substr string) bool {\n\treturn len(s) >= len(substr) && (s == substr || len(s) > 0 && containsStr(s, substr))\n}\n\nfunc containsStr(s, substr string) bool {\n\tfor i := 0; i <= len(s)-len(substr); i++ {\n\t\tif s[i:i+len(substr)] == substr {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

All tasks are complete. Here's a summary of what was implemented:

**OpenCode agent package** (`cmd/entire/cli/agent/opencode/`):
- `types.go` — Hook input structs, transcript types, tool constants
- `opencode.go` — Full Agent interface + TranscriptAnalyzer + TokenCalculator
- `lifecycle.go` — `ParseHookEvent()` mapping 6 hooks to event types + compile-time assertions
- `hooks.go` — HookSupport with embedded TypeScript plugin install/uninstall
- `entire.ts` — OpenCode plugin (session.created, message.updated, session.idle, session.compacted, tool hooks)
- `lifecycle_test.go`, `opencode_test.go`, `hooks_test.go` — Full test coverage

**Framework updates**:
- `registry.go` — Added `AgentNameOpenCode`/`AgentTypeOpenCode` constants
- `hooks_cmd.go` — Blank import for auto-registration
- `hook_registry.go` — OpenCode task hooks in `getHookType()`
- `setup.go` — Refactored from hardcoded agent lists to generic `agent.List()` loops
- `summarize/summarize.go` — Added `AgentTypeOpenCode` to JSONL parser switch

**Key design**: No custom handler code — the agent implements `ParseHookEvent()` and the lifecycle dispatcher handles all orchestration, exactly as the agent-guide prescribes.
